\displaystyle=\operatorname{curl}\left(Q\left[X\cdot\nabla K_{\varepsilon}[Y]+(DX)K_{\varepsilon}[Y]\right]\right)

(3.4)

\displaystyle=\operatorname{curl}\left(P_{\varepsilon}\left[Q\left[X\cdot\nabla K_{\varepsilon}[Y]+(DX)K_{\varepsilon}[Y]\right]\right]\right).

In particular if both
X
and
Y
are independent from the third variable, it holds

(3.5)

\displaystyle X\cdot\nabla Y^{H}-Y\cdot\nabla X^{H}=-\nabla^{\perp}_{H}\left(X\cdot\nabla K^{3}_{\varepsilon}[Y]\right),

where we denoted by
K^{3}_{\varepsilon}[Y]
the third component of
K_{\varepsilon}[Y]
.

Now we have all the elements to provide the necessary a priori estimates.

Lemma 3.3
.

Assuming
2.6
, for each
\xi_{1},\ \xi_{2}>0
we have

\displaystyle d\left\|{{A}^{\varepsilon}_{t}}^{\prime}\right\|_{\varepsilon}^{2}+2\eta\left\|\nabla{{A}^{\varepsilon}_{t}}^{\prime}\right\|^{2}_{\varepsilon}dt

\displaystyle\leq 2\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k\in\mathbb{Z}^{3,N}_{0}\\
j\in\{1,2\}\end{subarray}}\langle D\sigma_{k,j}^{\varepsilon}{{A}^{\varepsilon}_{t}}^{\prime},{{A}^{\varepsilon}_{t}}^{\prime}\rangle_{\varepsilon}dW^{k,j}_{t}

\displaystyle+2\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k\in\mathbb{Z}^{3,N}_{0}\\
k_{3}\neq 0,\hskip 8.19447ptj\in\{1,2\}\end{subarray}}\langle\sigma_{k,j}^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla\overline{A^{\varepsilon}_{t}}+D\sigma_{k,j}^{\varepsilon}\overline{A^{\varepsilon}_{t}},{{A}^{\varepsilon}_{t}}^{\prime}\rangle_{\varepsilon}dW^{k,j}_{t}

\displaystyle+2\left(\frac{\zeta_{H,0}}{C_{1,H}^{2}}+\frac{\lvert\rho\rvert}{\xi_{1}}\frac{\left(\zeta_{H,0}+\zeta_{H,2}\right)}{C_{1,H}^{2}}+O(N^{-1})\right)\left\|\nabla{{A}^{\varepsilon}_{t}}^{\prime}\right\|^{2}_{\varepsilon}dt

(3.6)

\displaystyle+\left(6\frac{\zeta_{\beta}\zeta_{H,0}}{N^{\beta-2}C_{V}^{2}}+O(N^{1-\beta})\right)\lVert\nabla\overline{A^{\varepsilon}_{t}}\rVert_{\varepsilon}^{2}dt+\left(6\frac{\zeta_{\beta-2}\zeta_{H,0}}{N^{\beta-4}C_{V}^{2}}+O(N^{3-\beta})\right)\left\|\overline{A^{\varepsilon}_{t}}\right\|_{\varepsilon}^{2}dt,

\displaystyle d\left\|{{B}^{3,\varepsilon}_{t}}^{\prime}\right\|^{2}_{\varepsilon}+2\eta\left\|\nabla{{B}^{3,\varepsilon}_{t}}^{\prime}\right\|^{2}_{\varepsilon}dt

\displaystyle\leq-2\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k\in\mathbb{Z}^{3,N}_{0}\\
j\in\{1,2\}\end{subarray}}\langle{{B}^{\varepsilon}_{t}}^{\prime}\cdot\nabla\sigma_{k,j}^{3,\varepsilon},{{B}^{3,\varepsilon}_{t}}^{\prime}\rangle_{\varepsilon}dW^{k,j}_{t}+2\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k\in\mathbb{Z}^{3,N}_{0}\\
k_{3}\neq 0,\hskip 8.19447ptj\in\{1,2\}\end{subarray}}\langle\sigma_{k,j}^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla\overline{B^{3,\varepsilon}_{t}}-\overline{B^{\varepsilon}_{t}}\cdot\nabla\sigma_{k,j}^{3,\varepsilon},{{B}^{3,\varepsilon}_{t}}^{\prime}\rangle_{\varepsilon}dW^{k,j}_{t}

\displaystyle+2\frac{\lvert\rho\rvert}{\xi_{2}}\left(\frac{\zeta_{H,2}}{C_{1,H}^{2}}+\sqrt{2}\frac{\zeta_{H,\gamma/2}}{C_{1,H}C_{2,H}}+O(N^{-1})\right)\left\|\nabla{{B}^{3,\varepsilon}_{t}}^{\prime}\right\|_{\varepsilon}^{2}dt

\displaystyle+2\left(\frac{\zeta_{H,\gamma-2}}{N^{\gamma-4}C_{2,H}^{2}}+2\frac{\zeta_{\beta-2}\zeta_{H,0}}{N^{\beta-4}C_{V}^{2}}+\frac{\lvert\rho\rvert\xi_{2}\zeta_{H,\gamma-2}}{N^{\gamma-4}C_{2,H}^{2}}+\sqrt{2}\lvert\rho\rvert\xi_{2}\frac{\zeta_{H,\gamma/2}}{N^{\gamma-4}C_{1,H}C_{2,H}}+O(N^{3-\gamma}+N^{3-\beta})\right)\left\|\nabla{{A}^{\varepsilon}_{t}}^{\prime}\right\|^{2}_{\varepsilon}dt

\displaystyle+2\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k\in\mathbb{Z}^{3,N}_{0}\\
k_{3}\neq 0,\hskip 8.19447ptj\in\{1,2\}\end{subarray}}\left\|\sigma_{k,j}^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla\overline{B^{3,\varepsilon}_{t}}\right\|^{2}_{\varepsilon}dt+\left(4\frac{\zeta_{\beta-2}\zeta_{H,0}}{N^{\beta-4}C_{V}^{2}}+O(N^{3-\beta})\right)\left\|\overline{B^{\varepsilon}_{t}}\right\|^{2}_{\varepsilon}dt

(3.7)

\displaystyle-2\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k\in\mathbb{Z}^{3,N}_{0}\\
k_{3}\neq 0,\hskip 8.19447ptj\in\{1,2\}\end{subarray}}\left\|\overline{\sigma_{k,j}^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla{{B}^{3,\varepsilon}_{t}}^{\prime}-{{B}^{\varepsilon}_{t}}^{\prime}\cdot\nabla\sigma_{k,j}^{3,\varepsilon}}\right\|_{\varepsilon}^{2}dt.

Proof.

Since by
3.1
it holds

(3.8)

\displaystyle\left\|{{A}^{\varepsilon}_{{}_{t}}}^{\prime}\right\|_{\varepsilon}^{2}=\left\|(-\Delta)^{-1/2}{{B}^{\varepsilon}_{{}_{t}}}^{\prime}\right\|_{\varepsilon}^{2},\quad\left\|\nabla{{A}^{\varepsilon}_{{}_{t}}}^{\prime}\right\|_{\varepsilon}^{2}=\left\|{{B}^{\varepsilon}_{{}_{t}}}^{\prime}\right\|_{\varepsilon}^{2},

we apply Itô formula to
\left\|(-\Delta)^{-1/2}{{B}^{\varepsilon}_{{}_{t}}}^{\prime}\right\|_{\varepsilon}^{2}
obtaining

\displaystyle d\left\|{{A}^{\varepsilon}_{t}}^{\prime}\right\|^{2}_{\varepsilon}=dW^{{}^{\prime}}_{t}+2(-I_{t}^{1}+I_{t}^{2}+I_{t}^{3}+I_{t}^{4}+I^{5}_{t}+I^{6}_{t})dt,

where we denote by

\displaystyle dW^{\prime}_{t}

\displaystyle=2\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k\in\mathbb{Z}^{3,N}_{0}\\
k_{3}=0,\hskip 8.19447ptj\in\{1,2\}\end{subarray}}\langle\mathcal{L}_{\sigma_{k,j}^{\varepsilon}}{{B}^{\varepsilon}_{t}}^{\prime},(-\Delta)^{-1}{{B}^{\varepsilon}_{t}}^{\prime}\rangle_{\varepsilon}dW^{k,j}_{t}+2\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k\in\mathbb{Z}^{3,N}_{0}\\
k_{3}\neq 0,\hskip 8.19447ptj\in\{1,2\}\end{subarray}}\langle\mathcal{L}_{\sigma_{k,j}^{\varepsilon}}\overline{B^{\varepsilon}_{t}},(-\Delta)^{-1}{{B}^{\varepsilon}_{t}}^{\prime}\rangle_{\varepsilon}dW^{k,j}_{t}

\displaystyle+2\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k\in\mathbb{Z}^{3,N}_{0}\\
k_{3}\neq 0,\hskip 8.19447ptj\in\{1,2\}\end{subarray}}\langle\left(\mathcal{L}_{\sigma_{k,j}^{\varepsilon}}{{B}^{\varepsilon}_{t}}^{\prime}\right)^{\prime},(-\Delta)^{-1}{{B}^{\varepsilon}_{t}}^{\prime}\rangle_{\varepsilon}dW^{k,j}_{t},

\displaystyle I^{1}_{t}

\displaystyle=\eta\langle\nabla{{B}^{\varepsilon}_{t}}^{\prime},(-\Delta)^{-1}\nabla{{B}^{\varepsilon}_{t}}^{\prime}\rangle_{\varepsilon}+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k\in\mathbb{Z}^{3,N}_{0}\\
j\in\{1,2\}\end{subarray}}\langle{{B}^{\varepsilon}_{t}}^{\prime},(-\Delta)^{-1}\operatorname{div}(\sigma_{k,j}^{\varepsilon}\otimes\sigma_{-k,j}^{\varepsilon}\nabla{{B}^{\varepsilon}_{t}}^{\prime})\rangle_{\varepsilon},

\displaystyle I^{2}_{t}

\displaystyle=\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k\in\mathbb{Z}^{3,N}_{0}\\
k_{3}=0,\hskip 8.19447ptj\in\{1,2\}\end{subarray}}\langle\mathcal{L}_{\sigma_{k,j}^{\varepsilon}}{{B}^{\varepsilon}_{t}}^{\prime},(-\Delta)^{-1}\mathcal{L}_{\sigma_{-k,j}^{\varepsilon}}{{B}^{\varepsilon}_{t}}^{\prime}\rangle_{\varepsilon},

\displaystyle I^{3}_{t}

\displaystyle=\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k\in\mathbb{Z}^{3,N}_{0}\\
k_{3}\neq 0,\hskip 8.19447ptj\in\{1,2\}\end{subarray}}\langle\mathcal{L}_{\sigma_{k,j}^{\varepsilon}}\overline{B^{\varepsilon}_{t}},(-\Delta)^{-1}\mathcal{L}_{\sigma_{-k,j}^{\varepsilon}}\overline{B^{\varepsilon}_{t}}\rangle_{\varepsilon},

\displaystyle I^{4}_{t}

\displaystyle=\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k\in\mathbb{Z}^{3,N}_{0}\\
k_{3}\neq 0,\hskip 8.19447ptj\in\{1,2\}\end{subarray}}\langle\left(\mathcal{L}_{\sigma_{k,j}^{\varepsilon}}{{B}^{\varepsilon}_{t}}^{\prime}\right)^{\prime},(-\Delta)^{-1}\left(\mathcal{L}_{\sigma_{-k,j}^{\varepsilon}}{{B}^{\varepsilon}_{t}}^{\prime}\right)^{\prime}\rangle_{\varepsilon},

\displaystyle I^{5}_{t}

\displaystyle=\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k\in\mathbb{Z}^{3,N}_{0}\\
k_{3}\neq 0,\hskip 8.19447ptj\in\{1,2\}\end{subarray}}\langle\mathcal{L}_{\sigma_{k,j}^{\varepsilon}}\overline{B^{\varepsilon}_{t}},(-\Delta)^{-1}\left(\mathcal{L}_{\sigma_{-k,j}^{\varepsilon}}{{B}^{\varepsilon}_{t}}^{\prime}\right)^{\prime}\rangle_{\varepsilon},

\displaystyle I_{t}^{6}

\displaystyle=\langle\Lambda^{\varepsilon}_{\rho}{{B}^{\varepsilon}_{t}}^{\prime},(-\Delta)^{-1}{{B}^{\varepsilon}_{t}}^{\prime}\rangle_{\varepsilon}+\rho\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k\in\mathbb{Z}^{3,N}_{0}\\
k_{3}=0\end{subarray}}\langle\mathcal{L}_{\sigma_{k,1}^{\varepsilon}},(-\Delta)^{-1}\mathcal{L}_{\sigma_{-k,2}^{\varepsilon}}\rangle_{\varepsilon}+\langle\mathcal{L}_{\sigma_{k,2}^{\varepsilon}},(-\Delta)^{-1}\mathcal{L}_{\sigma_{-k,1}^{\varepsilon}}\rangle_{\varepsilon}.

In order to study the terms involved in previous relation we recall that by
3.2

\displaystyle\operatorname{curl}(\sigma_{k,j}^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla{{A}^{\varepsilon}_{t}}^{\prime}+D\sigma_{k,j}^{\varepsilon}{{A}^{\varepsilon}_{t}}^{\prime})=\sigma_{k,j}^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla{{B}^{\varepsilon}_{t}}^{\prime}-{{B}^{\varepsilon}_{t}}^{\prime}\cdot\nabla\sigma_{k,j}^{\varepsilon}

and analogously for the others. First we start rewritng the martingale term. By previous relation, exploiting the fact that
\operatorname{curl}\operatorname{curl}f=-\Delta f
if
f
is a divergence free vector field and derivatives and the operator
(-\Delta)^{-1}
commute in the periodic framework we get

\displaystyle dW^{\prime}_{t}

\displaystyle=2\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k\in\mathbb{Z}^{3,N}_{0}\\
j\in\{1,2\}\end{subarray}}\langle\mathcal{L}_{\sigma_{k,j}^{\varepsilon}}{{B}^{\varepsilon}_{t}}^{\prime},(-\Delta)^{-1}{{B}^{\varepsilon}_{t}}^{\prime}\rangle_{\varepsilon}dW^{k,j}_{t}+2\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k\in\mathbb{Z}^{3,N}_{0}\\
k_{3}\neq 0,\hskip 8.19447ptj\in\{1,2\}\end{subarray}}\langle\mathcal{L}_{\sigma_{k,j}^{\varepsilon}}\overline{B^{\varepsilon}_{t}},(-\Delta)^{-1}{{B}^{\varepsilon}_{t}}^{\prime}\rangle_{\varepsilon}dW^{k,j}_{t}

\displaystyle=2\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k\in\mathbb{Z}^{3,N}_{0}\\
j\in\{1,2\}\end{subarray}}\langle\operatorname{curl}\left(\sigma_{k,j}^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla{{A}^{\varepsilon}_{t}}^{\prime}+D\sigma_{k,j}^{\varepsilon}{{A}^{\varepsilon}_{t}}^{\prime}\right),(-\Delta)^{-1}\operatorname{curl}{{A}^{\varepsilon}_{t}}^{\prime}\rangle_{\varepsilon}dW^{k,j}_{t}

\displaystyle+2\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k\in\mathbb{Z}^{3,N}_{0}\\
k_{3}\neq 0,\hskip 8.19447ptj\in\{1,2\}\end{subarray}}\langle\operatorname{curl}\left(\sigma_{k,j}^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla\overline{A^{\varepsilon}_{t}}+D\sigma_{k,j}^{\varepsilon}\overline{A^{\varepsilon}_{t}}\right),(-\Delta)^{-1}\operatorname{curl}{{A}^{\varepsilon}_{t}}^{\prime}\rangle_{\varepsilon}dW^{k,j}_{t}

\displaystyle=2\left(\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k\in\mathbb{Z}^{3,N}_{0}\\
j\in\{1,2\}\end{subarray}}\langle\sigma_{k,j}^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla{{A}^{\varepsilon}_{t}}^{\prime}+D\sigma_{k,j}^{\varepsilon}{{A}^{\varepsilon}_{t}}^{\prime},{{A}^{\varepsilon}_{t}}^{\prime}\rangle_{\varepsilon}dW^{k,j}_{t}+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k\in\mathbb{Z}^{3,N}_{0}\\
k_{3}\neq 0,\hskip 8.19447ptj\in\{1,2\}\end{subarray}}\langle\sigma_{k,j}^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla\overline{A^{\varepsilon}_{t}}+D\sigma_{k,j}^{\varepsilon}\overline{A^{\varepsilon}_{t}},{{A}^{\varepsilon}_{t}}^{\prime}\rangle_{\varepsilon}dW^{k,j}_{t}\right)

(3.9)

\displaystyle=2\left(\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k\in\mathbb{Z}^{3,N}_{0}\\
j\in\{1,2\}\end{subarray}}\langle D\sigma_{k,j}^{\varepsilon}{{A}^{\varepsilon}_{t}}^{\prime},{{A}^{\varepsilon}_{t}}^{\prime}\rangle_{\varepsilon}dW^{k,j}_{t}+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k\in\mathbb{Z}^{3,N}_{0}\\
k_{3}\neq 0,\hskip 8.19447ptj\in\{1,2\}\end{subarray}}\langle\sigma_{k,j}^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla\overline{A^{\varepsilon}_{t}}+D\sigma_{k,j}^{\varepsilon}\overline{A^{\varepsilon}_{t}},{{A}^{\varepsilon}_{t}}^{\prime}\rangle_{\varepsilon}dW^{k,j}_{t}\right).

Now we can analyze the terms
I^{1}_{t},\ I^{2}_{t},\ I^{3}_{t},\ I^{4}_{t},\ I^{5}_{t},\ I^{6}_{t}
. First we observe that due to the fact that derivatives and the operator
(-\Delta)^{-1}
commute in the periodic framework we easily obtain

(3.10)

\displaystyle I^{1}_{t}

\displaystyle=\eta\left\|\nabla{{A}^{\varepsilon}_{t}}^{\prime}\right\|_{\varepsilon}^{2}+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k\in\mathbb{Z}^{3,N}_{0}\\
j\in\{1,2\}\end{subarray}}\left\|\sigma_{k,j}^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla{{A}^{\varepsilon}_{t}}^{\prime}\right\|^{2}_{\varepsilon}.

Exploiting the fact that
\operatorname{curl}\operatorname{curl}f=-\Delta f
if
f
is a divergence free vector field we get

\displaystyle I^{2}_{t}

\displaystyle=\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k\in\mathbb{Z}^{3,N}_{0}\\
k_{3}=0,\hskip 8.19447ptj\in\{1,2\}\end{subarray}}\langle\operatorname{curl}(\sigma_{k,j}^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla{{A}^{\varepsilon}_{t}}^{\prime}+D\sigma_{k,j}^{\varepsilon}{{A}^{\varepsilon}_{t}}^{\prime}),(-\Delta)^{-1}\left(\operatorname{curl}(\sigma_{-k,j}^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla{{A}^{\varepsilon}_{t}}^{\prime}+D\sigma_{-k,j}^{\varepsilon}{{A}^{\varepsilon}_{t}}^{\prime})\right)\rangle_{\varepsilon}

\displaystyle=\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k\in\mathbb{Z}^{3,N}_{0}\\
k_{3}=0,\hskip 8.19447ptj\in\{1,2\}\end{subarray}}\langle\operatorname{curl}\left(P_{\varepsilon}\left[Q\left[\sigma_{k,j}^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla{{A}^{\varepsilon}_{t}}^{\prime}+D\sigma_{k,j}^{\varepsilon}{{A}^{\varepsilon}_{t}}^{\prime})\right]\right]\right),(-\Delta)^{-1}\operatorname{curl}\left(P_{\varepsilon}\left[Q\left[\sigma_{k,j}^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla{{A}^{\varepsilon}_{t}}^{\prime}+D\sigma_{k,j}^{\varepsilon}{{A}^{\varepsilon}_{t}}^{\prime})\right]\right]\right)\rangle_{\varepsilon}

\displaystyle=\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k\in\mathbb{Z}^{3,N}_{0}\\
k_{3}=0,\hskip 8.19447ptj\in\{1,2\}\end{subarray}}\left\|P_{\varepsilon}\left[Q\left[\sigma_{k,j}^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla{{A}^{\varepsilon}_{t}}^{\prime}+D\sigma_{k,j}^{\varepsilon}{{A}^{\varepsilon}_{t}}^{\prime}\right]\right]\right\|^{2}_{\varepsilon}

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k\in\mathbb{Z}^{3,N}_{0}\\
k_{3}=0,\hskip 8.19447ptj\in\{1,2\}\end{subarray}}\left\|\sigma_{k,j}^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla{{A}^{\varepsilon}_{t}}^{\prime}+D\sigma_{k,j}^{\varepsilon}{{A}^{\varepsilon}_{t}}^{\prime}\right\|^{2}_{\varepsilon}=\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k\in\mathbb{Z}^{3,N}_{0}\\
k_{3}=0,\hskip 8.19447ptj\in\{1,2\}\end{subarray}}\left\|\sigma_{k,j}^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla{{A}^{\varepsilon}_{t}}^{\prime}\right\|^{2}_{\varepsilon}+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k\in\mathbb{Z}^{3,N}_{0}\\
k_{3}=0,\hskip 8.19447ptj\in\{1,2\}\end{subarray}}\left\|D\sigma_{k,j}^{\varepsilon}{{A}^{\varepsilon}_{t}}^{\prime}\right\|^{2}_{\varepsilon},

where in the last step we exploited (
2.17
). By Hölder inequality and Poincaré inequality (
2.5
) the last term in the relation above can be estimated by

\displaystyle\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k\in\mathbb{Z}^{3,N}_{0}\\
k_{3}=0,\hskip 8.19447ptj\in\{1,2\}\end{subarray}}\left\|D\sigma_{k,j}^{\varepsilon}{{A}^{\varepsilon}_{t}}^{\prime}\right\|^{2}_{\varepsilon}

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k\in\mathbb{Z}^{3,N}_{0}\\
k_{3}=0,\hskip 8.19447ptj\in\{1,2\}\end{subarray}}(\theta_{k,j}^{\varepsilon})^{2}\lvert k\rvert^{2}\left\|{{A}^{\varepsilon}_{t}}^{\prime}\right\|^{2}_{\varepsilon}

\displaystyle\leq\varepsilon^{2}\left\|\partial_{3}{{A}^{\varepsilon}_{t}}^{\prime}\right\|^{2}_{\varepsilon}\left(\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k\in\mathbb{Z}^{3,N}_{0}\\
k_{3}=0,\hskip 8.19447ptj=1\end{subarray}}(\theta_{k,j}^{\varepsilon})^{2}\lvert k\rvert^{2}+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k\in\mathbb{Z}^{3,N}_{0}\\
k_{3}=0,\hskip 8.19447ptj=2\end{subarray}}(\theta_{k,j}^{\varepsilon})^{2}\lvert k\rvert^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\varepsilon^{2}\left\|\nabla{{A}^{\varepsilon}_{t}}^{\prime}\right\|^{2}_{\varepsilon}\left(\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k_{H}\in\mathbb{Z}^{2}_{0}\\
N\leq\lvert k_{H}\rvert\leq 2N\end{subarray}}\frac{1}{C^{2}_{1,H}}+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k_{H}\in\mathbb{Z}^{2}_{0}\\
N\leq\lvert k_{H}\rvert\leq 2N\end{subarray}}\frac{1}{C^{2}_{2,H}\lvert k_{H}\rvert^{\gamma-2}}\right)

\displaystyle=\left\|\nabla{{A}^{\varepsilon}_{t}}^{\prime}\right\|^{2}_{\varepsilon}\left(\frac{\zeta^{N}_{H,0}}{C_{1,H}^{2}}+\frac{\zeta^{N}_{H,\gamma-2}}{N^{\gamma-2}C_{2,H}^{2}}\right)=\left\|\nabla{{A}^{\varepsilon}_{t}}^{\prime}\right\|^{2}_{\varepsilon}\left(\frac{\zeta_{H,0}}{C_{1,H}^{2}}+O(N^{-1})\right).

Therefore

(3.11)

\displaystyle I^{2}_{t}\leq\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k\in\mathbb{Z}^{3,N}_{0}\\
k_{3}=0,\hskip 8.19447ptj\in\{1,2\}\end{subarray}}\left\|\sigma_{k,j}^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla{{A}^{\varepsilon}_{t}}^{\prime}\right\|^{2}_{\varepsilon}+\left\|\nabla{{A}^{\varepsilon}_{t}}^{\prime}\right\|^{2}_{\varepsilon}\left(\frac{\zeta_{H,0}}{C_{1,H}^{2}}+O(N^{-1})\right).

Analogously we can show that

\displaystyle I^{3}_{t}

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k\in\mathbb{Z}^{3,N}_{0}\\
k_{3}\neq 0,\hskip 8.19447ptj\in\{1,2\}\end{subarray}}\left\|\sigma_{k,j}^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla\overline{A^{\varepsilon}_{t}}\right\|^{2}_{\varepsilon}+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k\in\mathbb{Z}^{3,N}_{0}\\
k_{3}\neq 0,\hskip 8.19447ptj\in\{1,2\}\end{subarray}}\left\|D\sigma_{k,j}^{\varepsilon}\overline{A^{\varepsilon}_{t}}\right\|^{2}_{\varepsilon}

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k\in\mathbb{Z}^{3,N}_{0}\\
k_{3}\neq 0,\hskip 8.19447ptj\in\{1,2\}\end{subarray}}\left\lvert\theta_{k,j}^{\varepsilon}\right\rvert^{2}\left\|\nabla\overline{A^{\varepsilon}_{t}}\right\|^{2}_{\varepsilon}+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k\in\mathbb{Z}^{3,N}_{0}\\
k_{3}\neq 0,\hskip 8.19447ptj\in\{1,2\}\end{subarray}}\left\lvert\theta_{k,j}^{\varepsilon}\right\rvert^{2}\lvert k\rvert^{2}\left\|\overline{A^{\varepsilon}_{t}}\right\|^{2}_{\varepsilon}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{(\zeta_{H,0}N^{2}+O(N))\lVert\nabla\overline{A^{\varepsilon}_{t}}\rVert_{\varepsilon}^{2}}{C_{V}^{2}}\sum_{k_{3}\in N\mathbb{Z}\smallsetminus\{0\}}\frac{1}{\lvert k_{3}\rvert^{\beta}}+\frac{(\zeta_{H,0}N^{2}+O(N))\left\|\overline{A^{\varepsilon}_{t}}\right\|_{\varepsilon}^{2}}{C_{V}^{2}}\sum_{k_{3}\in N\mathbb{Z}\smallsetminus\{0\}}\frac{1}{\lvert k_{3}\rvert^{\beta-2}}

(3.12)

\displaystyle=\left(2\frac{\zeta_{\beta}\zeta_{H,0}}{N^{\beta-2}C_{V}^{2}}+O(N^{1-\beta})\right)\lVert\nabla\overline{A^{\varepsilon}_{t}}\rVert_{\varepsilon}^{2}+\left(2\frac{\zeta_{\beta-2}\zeta_{H,0}}{N^{\beta-4}C_{V}^{2}}+O(N^{3-\beta})\right)\left\|\overline{A^{\varepsilon}_{t}}\right\|_{\varepsilon}^{2}.

The analysis of
I^{4}_{t}
is similar recalling that by
2.3
the operator
N_{\varepsilon}
and the
\operatorname{curl}
commute. Therefore we have, arguing as above,

\displaystyle I^{4}_{t}

\displaystyle=\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k\in\mathbb{Z}^{3,N}_{0}\\
k_{3}\neq 0,\hskip 8.19447ptj\in\{1,2\}\end{subarray}}\left\|P_{\varepsilon}\left[Q\left[\left(\sigma_{k,j}^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla{{A}^{\varepsilon}_{t}}^{\prime}+D\sigma_{k,j}^{\varepsilon}{{A}^{\varepsilon}_{t}}^{\prime}\right)^{\prime}\right]\right]\right\|^{2}_{\varepsilon}

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k\in\mathbb{Z}^{3,N}_{0}\\
k_{3}\neq 0,\hskip 8.19447ptj\in\{1,2\}\end{subarray}}\left\|\left(\sigma_{k,j}^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla{{A}^{\varepsilon}_{t}}^{\prime}+D\sigma_{k,j}^{\varepsilon}{{A}^{\varepsilon}_{t}}^{\prime}\right)^{\prime}\right\|^{2}_{\varepsilon}

\displaystyle=\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k\in\mathbb{Z}^{3,N}_{0}\\
k_{3}\neq 0,\hskip 8.19447ptj\in\{1,2\}\end{subarray}}\left\|\sigma_{k,j}^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla{{A}^{\varepsilon}_{t}}^{\prime}+D\sigma_{k,j}^{\varepsilon}{{A}^{\varepsilon}_{t}}^{\prime}\right\|^{2}_{\varepsilon}-\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k\in\mathbb{Z}^{3,N}_{0}\\
k_{3}\neq 0,\hskip 8.19447ptj\in\{1,2\}\end{subarray}}\left\|\overline{\sigma_{k,j}^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla{{A}^{\varepsilon}_{t}}^{\prime}+D\sigma_{k,j}^{\varepsilon}{{A}^{\varepsilon}_{t}}^{\prime}}\right\|^{2}_{\varepsilon}

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k\in\mathbb{Z}^{3,N}_{0}\\
k_{3}\neq 0,\hskip 8.19447ptj\in\{1,2\}\end{subarray}}\left\|\sigma_{k,j}^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla{{A}^{\varepsilon}_{t}}^{\prime}\right\|^{2}_{\varepsilon}+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k\in\mathbb{Z}^{3,N}_{0}\\
k_{3}\neq 0,\hskip 8.19447ptj\in\{1,2\}\end{subarray}}\left\|D\sigma_{k,j}^{\varepsilon}{{A}^{\varepsilon}_{t}}^{\prime}\right\|^{2}_{\varepsilon}.

In the last step we simply neglected the negative term and exploited (
2.17
). Arguing as when treating
I^{2}_{t}
, we can show by Hölder and Poincaré inequality in the thin layer (
2.5
) that

\displaystyle\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k\in\mathbb{Z}^{3,N}_{0}\\
k_{3}\neq 0,\hskip 8.19447ptj\in\{1,2\}\end{subarray}}\left\|D\sigma_{k,j}^{\varepsilon}{{A}^{\varepsilon}_{t}}^{\prime}\right\|^{2}_{\varepsilon}

\displaystyle\leq\varepsilon^{2}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k\in\mathbb{Z}^{3,N}_{0}\\
k_{3}\neq 0,\hskip 8.19447ptj\in\{1,2\}\\
N\leq\lvert k_{H}\rvert\leq 2N\end{subarray}}\frac{1}{C_{V}^{2}\lvert k\rvert^{\beta-2}}\left\|\nabla{{A}^{\varepsilon}_{t}}^{\prime}\right\|_{\varepsilon}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\left(\zeta_{H,0}+O(N^{-1})\right)\left\|\nabla{{A}^{\varepsilon}_{t}}^{\prime}\right\|_{\varepsilon}^{2}}{C_{V}^{2}}\sum_{k_{3}\in N\mathbb{Z}\smallsetminus\{0\}}\frac{1}{\lvert k_{3}\rvert^{\beta-2}}

\displaystyle=\left(2\frac{\zeta_{\beta-2}\zeta_{H,0}}{N^{\beta-2}C_{V}^{2}}+O(N^{1-\beta})\right)\left\|\nabla{{A}^{\varepsilon}_{t}}^{\prime}\right\|_{\varepsilon}^{2}.

In particular, it holds

(3.13)

\displaystyle I^{4}_{t}\leq\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k\in\mathbb{Z}^{3,N}_{0}\\
k_{3}\neq 0,\hskip 8.19447ptj\in\{1,2\}\end{subarray}}\left\|\sigma_{k,j}^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla{{A}^{\varepsilon}_{t}}^{\prime}\right\|^{2}_{\varepsilon}+\left(2\frac{\zeta_{\beta-2}\zeta_{H,0}}{N^{\beta-2}C_{V}^{2}}+O(N^{1-\beta})\right)\left\|\nabla{{A}^{\varepsilon}_{t}}^{\prime}\right\|_{\varepsilon}^{2}.

For what concerns
I^{5}_{t}
, applying Cauchy-Schwarz and Young’s inequalities we get

\displaystyle I^{5}_{t}

\displaystyle=\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k\in\mathbb{Z}^{3,N}_{0}\\
k_{3}\neq 0,\hskip 8.19447ptj\in\{1,2\}\end{subarray}}\langle P_{\varepsilon}\left[Q\left[\sigma_{k,j}^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla\overline{A^{\varepsilon}_{t}}+D\sigma_{k,j}^{\varepsilon}\overline{A^{\varepsilon}_{t}}\right]\right],P_{\varepsilon}\left[Q\left[\left(\sigma_{-k,j}^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla{{A}^{\varepsilon}_{t}}^{\prime}+D\sigma_{-k,j}^{\varepsilon}{{A}^{\varepsilon}_{t}}^{\prime}\right)^{\prime}\right]\right]\rangle_{\varepsilon}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{I^{3}_{t}+I^{4}_{t}}{2}

\displaystyle\leq\left(\frac{\zeta_{\beta}\zeta_{H,0}}{N^{\beta-2}C_{V}^{2}}+O(N^{1-\beta})\right)\lVert\nabla\overline{A^{\varepsilon}_{t}}\rVert_{\varepsilon}^{2}+\left(\frac{\zeta_{\beta-2}\zeta_{H,0}}{N^{\beta-4}C_{V}^{2}}+O(N^{3-\beta})\right)\left\|\overline{A^{\varepsilon}_{t}}\right\|_{\varepsilon}^{2}

\displaystyle+\left(\frac{\zeta_{\beta-2}\zeta_{H,0}}{N^{\beta-2}C_{V}^{2}}+O(N^{1-\beta})\right)\left\|\nabla{{A}^{\varepsilon}_{t}}^{\prime}\right\|_{\varepsilon}^{2}+\frac{1}{2}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k\in\mathbb{Z}^{3,N}_{0}\\
k_{3}\neq 0,\hskip 8.19447ptj\in\{1,2\}\end{subarray}}\left\|\sigma_{k,j}^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla{{A}^{\varepsilon}_{t}}^{\prime}\right\|^{2}_{\varepsilon}.

Let us analyze the last term. It holds

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k\in\mathbb{Z}^{3,N}_{0}\\
k_{3}\neq 0,\hskip 8.19447ptj\in\{1,2\}\end{subarray}}\left\|\sigma_{k,j}^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla{{A}^{\varepsilon}_{t}}^{\prime}\right\|^{2}_{\varepsilon}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\left\|\nabla{{A}^{\varepsilon}_{t}}^{\prime}\right\|_{\varepsilon}^{2}}{2C_{V}^{2}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k\in\mathbb{Z}^{3,N}_{0}\\
k_{3}\neq 0,\hskip 8.19447ptj\in\{1,2\}\\
N\leq\lvert k_{H}\rvert\leq 2N\end{subarray}}\frac{1}{\lvert k\rvert^{\beta}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\left(\zeta_{H,0}N^{2}+O(N)\right)\left\|\nabla{{A}^{\varepsilon}_{t}}^{\prime}\right\|_{\varepsilon}^{2}}{2C_{V}^{2}}\sum_{k_{3}\in N\mathbb{Z}\smallsetminus\{0\}}\frac{1}{\lvert k_{3}\rvert^{\beta}}

\displaystyle=\left(\frac{\zeta_{\beta}\zeta_{H,0}}{N^{\beta-2}C_{V}^{2}}+O(N^{1-\beta})\right)\left\|\nabla{{A}^{\varepsilon}_{t}}^{\prime}\right\|_{\varepsilon}^{2}.

In conclusion it holds

\displaystyle I^{5}_{t}

\displaystyle\leq\left(\frac{\zeta_{\beta}\zeta_{H,0}}{N^{\beta-2}C_{V}^{2}}+O(N^{1-\beta})\right)\lVert\nabla\overline{A^{\varepsilon}_{t}}\rVert_{\varepsilon}^{2}+\left(\frac{\zeta_{\beta-2}\zeta_{H,0}}{N^{\beta-4}C_{V}^{2}}+O(N^{3-\beta})\right)\left\|\overline{A^{\varepsilon}_{t}}\right\|_{\varepsilon}^{2}

(3.14)

\displaystyle+\left(2\frac{\zeta_{\beta}\zeta_{H,0}}{N^{\beta-2}C_{V}^{2}}+O(N^{1-\beta})\right)\left\|\nabla{{A}^{\varepsilon}_{t}}^{\prime}\right\|_{\varepsilon}^{2}.

Lastly we consider
I^{6}_{t}
:
\langle\Lambda^{\varepsilon}_{\rho}{{B}^{\varepsilon}_{t}}^{\prime},(-\Delta)^{-1}{{B}^{\varepsilon}_{t}}^{\prime}\rangle_{\varepsilon}
can be estimated easily due to
2.10
and (
3.1
), obtaining by Cauchy-Schwarz inequality

\displaystyle\langle\Lambda^{\varepsilon}_{\rho}{{B}^{\varepsilon}_{t}}^{\prime},(-\Delta)^{-1}{{B}^{\varepsilon}_{t}}^{\prime}\rangle_{\varepsilon}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2\sqrt{2}\lvert\rho\rvert\zeta^{N}_{H,\gamma/2}N^{2-\gamma/2}}{C_{1,H}C_{2,H}}\left\|(-\Delta)^{-1/2}{{B}^{\varepsilon}_{t}}^{\prime}\right\|_{\varepsilon}\left\|\nabla{{B}^{\varepsilon}_{t}}^{\prime}\right\|_{H^{-1}(\mathbb{T}^{3}_{\varepsilon})}

\displaystyle=\frac{2\sqrt{2}\lvert\rho\rvert\zeta^{N}_{H,\gamma/2}N^{2-\gamma/2}}{C_{1,H}C_{2,H}}\left\|{{A}^{\varepsilon}_{t}}^{\prime}\right\|_{\varepsilon}\left\|\nabla{{A}^{\varepsilon}_{t}}^{\prime}\right\|_{\varepsilon}.

The other terms can be treated easily by Cauchy-Schwartz inequality and
3.2
. Indeed for each
\xi_{1}>0
and
k\in\mathbb{Z}_{0}^{3,N}
such that
k_{3}=0
we have

\displaystyle\langle\mathcal{L}_{\sigma_{k,1}^{\varepsilon}},(-\Delta)^{-1}\mathcal{L}_{\sigma_{-k,2}^{\varepsilon}}\rangle_{\varepsilon}

\displaystyle=\langle\operatorname{curl}\left(\sigma_{k,1}^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla{{A}^{\varepsilon}_{t}}^{\prime}+D\sigma_{k,1}^{\varepsilon}{{A}^{\varepsilon}_{t}}^{\prime}\right),(-\Delta)^{-1}\operatorname{curl}\left(\sigma_{-k,2}^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla{{A}^{\varepsilon}_{t}}^{\prime}+D\sigma_{-k,2}^{\varepsilon}{{A}^{\varepsilon}_{t}}^{\prime}\right)\rangle_{\varepsilon}

\displaystyle=\langle\operatorname{curl}\left(P_{\varepsilon}[Q[\sigma_{k,1}^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla{{A}^{\varepsilon}_{t}}^{\prime}+D\sigma_{k,1}^{\varepsilon}{{A}^{\varepsilon}_{t}}^{\prime}]]\right),(-\Delta)^{-1}\operatorname{curl}\left(P_{\varepsilon}[Q[\sigma_{-k,2}^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla{{A}^{\varepsilon}_{t}}^{\prime}+D\sigma_{-k,2}^{\varepsilon}{{A}^{\varepsilon}_{t}}^{\prime}]]\right)\rangle_{\varepsilon}

\displaystyle=\langle\left(P_{\varepsilon}[Q[\sigma_{k,1}^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla{{A}^{\varepsilon}_{t}}^{\prime}+D\sigma_{k,1}^{\varepsilon}{{A}^{\varepsilon}_{t}}^{\prime}]]\right),\left(P_{\varepsilon}[Q[\sigma_{-k,2}^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla{{A}^{\varepsilon}_{t}}^{\prime}+D\sigma_{-k,2}^{\varepsilon}{{A}^{\varepsilon}_{t}}^{\prime}]]\right)\rangle_{\varepsilon}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\left\|\sigma_{k,1}^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla{{A}^{\varepsilon}_{t}}^{\prime}+D\sigma_{k,1}^{\varepsilon}{{A}^{\varepsilon}_{t}}^{\prime}\right\|_{\varepsilon}^{2}}{2\xi_{1}}+\frac{\xi_{1}\left\|\sigma_{k,2}^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla{{A}^{\varepsilon}_{t}}^{\prime}+D\sigma_{k,2}^{\varepsilon}{{A}^{\varepsilon}_{t}}^{\prime}\right\|_{\varepsilon}^{2}}{2}.

So far, arguing when treating
I^{2}_{t}
, we proved that for each
\xi_{1}>0

\displaystyle I^{6}_{t}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2\sqrt{2}\lvert\rho\rvert\zeta^{N}_{H,\gamma/2}}{N^{\gamma/2-2}C_{1,H}C_{2,H}}\left\|{{A}^{\varepsilon}_{t}}^{\prime}\right\|_{\varepsilon}\left\|\nabla{{A}^{\varepsilon}_{t}}^{\prime}\right\|_{\varepsilon}+\frac{\lvert\rho\rvert}{\xi_{1}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k\in\mathbb{Z}^{3,N}_{0}\\
k_{3}=0,\hskip 8.19447ptj=1\end{subarray}}\left\|\sigma_{k,j}^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla{{A}^{\varepsilon}_{t}}^{\prime}\right\|^{2}_{\varepsilon}

(3.15)

\displaystyle+\lvert\rho\rvert\xi_{1}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k\in\mathbb{Z}^{3,N}_{0}\\
k_{3}=0,\hskip 8.19447ptj=2\end{subarray}}\left\|\sigma_{k,j}^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla{{A}^{\varepsilon}_{t}}^{\prime}\right\|^{2}_{\varepsilon}+\left\|\nabla{{A}^{\varepsilon}_{t}}^{\prime}\right\|_{\varepsilon}^{2}\left(\frac{\lvert\rho\rvert\zeta_{H,0}}{\xi_{1}C_{1,H}^{2}}+O(N^{-1})\right).

Let us analyze
\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k\in\mathbb{Z}^{3,N}_{0}\\
k_{3}=0,\hskip 8.19447ptj=1\end{subarray}}\left\|\sigma_{k,j}^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla{{A}^{\varepsilon}_{t}}^{\prime}\right\|^{2}_{\varepsilon}
and
\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k\in\mathbb{Z}^{3,N}_{0}\\
k_{3}=0,\hskip 8.19447ptj=2\end{subarray}}\left\|\sigma_{k,j}^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla{{A}^{\varepsilon}_{t}}^{\prime}\right\|^{2}_{\varepsilon}
. We have

\displaystyle\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k\in\mathbb{Z}^{3,N}_{0}\\
k_{3}=0,\hskip 8.19447ptj=1\end{subarray}}\left\|\sigma_{k,j}^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla{{A}^{\varepsilon}_{t}}^{\prime}\right\|^{2}_{\varepsilon}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\left\|\nabla{{A}^{\varepsilon}_{t}}^{\prime}\right\|_{\varepsilon}^{2}}{C_{1,H}^{2}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k_{H}\in\mathbb{Z}^{2}_{0}\\
N\leq\lvert k_{H}\rvert\leq 2N\end{subarray}}\frac{1}{\lvert k_{H}\rvert^{2}}

\displaystyle=\left(\frac{\zeta_{H,2}}{C_{1,H}^{2}}+O(N^{-1})\right)\left\|\nabla{{A}^{\varepsilon}_{t}}^{\prime}\right\|_{\varepsilon}^{2}

and analogously

\displaystyle\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k\in\mathbb{Z}^{3,N}_{0}\\
k_{3}=0,\hskip 8.19447ptj=2\end{subarray}}\left\|\sigma_{k,j}^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla{{A}^{\varepsilon}_{t}}^{\prime}\right\|^{2}_{\varepsilon}

\displaystyle\leq\left(\frac{\zeta_{H,\gamma}}{N^{\gamma-2}C_{1,H}^{2}}+O(N^{1-\gamma})\right)\left\|\nabla{{A}^{\varepsilon}_{t}}^{\prime}\right\|_{\varepsilon}^{2}.

In conclusion, applying Poincaré inequality (
2.5
) to (
3
), it holds

(3.16)

\displaystyle I^{6}_{t}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\lvert\rho\rvert}{\xi_{1}}\left(\frac{\zeta_{H,0}+\zeta_{H,2}}{C_{1,H}^{2}}+O(N^{-1})\right)\left\|\nabla{{A}^{\varepsilon}_{t}}^{\prime}\right\|_{\varepsilon}^{2}.

Combining (
3
), (
3.10
), (
3.11
), (
3
), (

), (

), (

) we get relation (
3.3
).

Now we move on to the analysis of
\left\|{{B}^{3,\varepsilon}_{t}}^{\prime}\right\|^{2}_{\varepsilon}
which is similar, actually easier, than the previous one. Indeed, by Itô formula it holds:

\displaystyle d\left\|{{B}^{3,\varepsilon}_{t}}^{\prime}\right\|^{2}_{\varepsilon}=d\tilde{W}^{\prime}_{t}+2(-J^{1}_{t}+J^{2}_{t}+J^{3}_{t}+J^{4}_{t}+J^{5}_{t}+J^{6}_{t})dt,

where we denote by

\displaystyle d\tilde{W}^{\prime}_{t}

\displaystyle=2\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k\in\mathbb{Z}^{3,N}_{0}\\
k_{3}=0,\hskip 8.19447ptj\in\{1,2\}\end{subarray}}\langle\sigma_{k,j}^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla{{B}^{3,\varepsilon}_{t}}^{\prime}-{{B}^{\varepsilon}_{t}}^{\prime}\cdot\nabla\sigma_{k,j}^{3,\varepsilon},{{B}^{3,\varepsilon}_{t}}^{\prime}\rangle_{\varepsilon}dW^{k,j}_{t}

\displaystyle+2\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k\in\mathbb{Z}^{3,N}_{0}\\
k_{3}\neq 0,\hskip 8.19447ptj\in\{1,2\}\end{subarray}}\langle\sigma_{k,j}^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla\overline{B^{3,\varepsilon}_{t}}-\overline{B^{\varepsilon}_{t}}\cdot\nabla\sigma_{k,j}^{3,\varepsilon},{{B}^{3,\varepsilon}_{t}}^{\prime}\rangle_{\varepsilon}dW^{k,j}_{t}

\displaystyle+2\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k\in\mathbb{Z}^{3,N}_{0}\\
k_{3}\neq 0,\hskip 8.19447ptj\in\{1,2\}\end{subarray}}\langle\left(\sigma_{k,j}^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla{{B}^{3,\varepsilon}_{t}}^{\prime}-{{B}^{\varepsilon}_{t}}^{\prime}\cdot\nabla\sigma_{k,j}^{3,\varepsilon}\right)^{\prime},{{B}^{3,\varepsilon}_{t}}^{\prime}\rangle_{\varepsilon}dW^{k,j}_{t},

\displaystyle J^{1}_{t}

\displaystyle=\eta\left\|\nabla{{B}^{3,\varepsilon}_{t}}^{\prime}\right\|_{\varepsilon}^{2}+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k\in\mathbb{Z}^{3,N}_{0}\\
j\in\{1,2\}\end{subarray}}\left\|\sigma_{k,j}^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla{{B}^{3,\varepsilon}_{t}}^{\prime}\right\|_{\varepsilon}^{2},

\displaystyle J^{2}_{t}

\displaystyle=\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k\in\mathbb{Z}^{3,N}_{0}\\
k_{3}=0,\hskip 8.19447ptj\in\{1,2\}\end{subarray}}\langle\sigma_{k,j}^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla{{B}^{3,\varepsilon}_{t}}^{\prime}-{{B}^{\varepsilon}_{t}}^{\prime}\cdot\nabla\sigma_{k,j}^{3,\varepsilon},\sigma_{-k,j}^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla{{B}^{3,\varepsilon}_{t}}^{\prime}-{{B}^{\varepsilon}_{t}}^{\prime}\cdot\nabla\sigma_{-k,j}^{3,\varepsilon}\rangle_{\varepsilon},

\displaystyle J^{3}_{t}

\displaystyle=\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k\in\mathbb{Z}^{3,N}_{0}\\
k_{3}\neq 0,\hskip 8.19447ptj\in\{1,2\}\end{subarray}}\langle\sigma_{k,j}^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla\overline{B^{3,\varepsilon}_{t}}-\overline{B^{\varepsilon}_{t}}\cdot\nabla\sigma_{k,j}^{3,\varepsilon},\sigma_{-k,j}^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla\overline{B^{3,\varepsilon}_{t}}-\overline{B^{\varepsilon}_{t}}\cdot\nabla\sigma_{-k,j}^{3,\varepsilon}\rangle_{\varepsilon},

\displaystyle J^{4}_{t}

\displaystyle=\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k\in\mathbb{Z}^{3,N}_{0}\\
k_{3}\neq 0,\hskip 8.19447ptj\in\{1,2\}\end{subarray}}\langle\left(\sigma_{k,j}^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla{{B}^{3,\varepsilon}_{t}}^{\prime}-{{B}^{\varepsilon}_{t}}^{\prime}\cdot\nabla\sigma_{k,j}^{3,\varepsilon}\right)^{\prime},\left(\sigma_{-k,j}^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla{{B}^{3,\varepsilon}_{t}}^{\prime}-{{B}^{\varepsilon}_{t}}^{\prime}\cdot\nabla\sigma_{-k,j}^{3,\varepsilon}\right)^{\prime}\rangle_{\varepsilon},

\displaystyle J^{5}_{t}

\displaystyle=\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k\in\mathbb{Z}^{3,N}_{0}\\
k_{3}\neq 0,\hskip 8.19447ptj\in\{1,2\}\end{subarray}}\langle\sigma_{k,j}^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla\overline{B^{3,\varepsilon}_{t}}-\overline{B^{\varepsilon}_{t}}\cdot\nabla\sigma_{k,j}^{3,\varepsilon},\left(\sigma_{-k,j}^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla{{B}^{3,\varepsilon}_{t}}^{\prime}-{{B}^{\varepsilon}_{t}}^{\prime}\cdot\nabla\sigma_{-k,j}^{3,\varepsilon}\right)^{\prime}\rangle_{\varepsilon},

\displaystyle J^{6}_{t}

\displaystyle=-\langle\operatorname{div}_{H}\left(\mathcal{R}_{\varepsilon,\gamma}{{B}^{H,\varepsilon}_{t}}^{\prime}\right),{{B}^{3,\varepsilon}_{t}}^{\prime}\rangle_{\varepsilon}

\displaystyle+\rho\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k\in\mathbb{Z}^{3,N}_{0}\\
k_{3}=0\end{subarray}}\langle\sigma_{k,1}^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla{{B}^{3,\varepsilon}_{t}}^{\prime},\sigma_{-k,2}^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla{{B}^{3,\varepsilon}_{t}}^{\prime}-{{B}^{\varepsilon}_{t}}^{\prime}\cdot\nabla\sigma_{-k,2}^{3,\varepsilon}\rangle_{\varepsilon}+\langle\sigma_{k,2}^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla{{B}^{3,\varepsilon}_{t}}^{\prime}-{{B}^{\varepsilon}_{t}}^{\prime}\cdot\nabla\sigma_{k,2}^{3,\varepsilon},\sigma_{-k,1}^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla{{B}^{3,\varepsilon}_{t}}^{\prime}\rangle_{\varepsilon}.

Since the
\sigma_{k,j}^{\varepsilon}
are divergence free, the martingale term is equal to

(3.17)

\displaystyle d\tilde{W}^{\prime}_{t}

\displaystyle=-2\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k\in\mathbb{Z}^{3,N}_{0}\\
j\in\{1,2\}\end{subarray}}\langle{{B}^{\varepsilon}_{t}}^{\prime}\cdot\nabla\sigma_{k,j}^{3,\varepsilon},{{B}^{3,\varepsilon}_{t}}^{\prime}\rangle_{\varepsilon}dW^{k,j}_{t}+2\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k\in\mathbb{Z}^{3,N}_{0}\\
k_{3}\neq 0,\hskip 8.19447ptj\in\{1,2\}\end{subarray}}\langle\sigma_{k,j}^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla\overline{B^{3,\varepsilon}_{t}}-\overline{B^{\varepsilon}_{t}}\cdot\nabla\sigma_{k,j}^{3,\varepsilon},{{B}^{3,\varepsilon}_{t}}^{\prime}\rangle_{\varepsilon}dW^{k,j}_{t}.

The analysis of
J^{2}_{t},\ J^{3}_{t},\ J^{4}_{t},\ J^{5}_{t}
is similar to the corresponding terms when estimating
\left\|{{A}^{\varepsilon}_{t}}^{\prime}\right\|_{\varepsilon}^{2}
and is based on the fact that the mixed products in each inner product are equal to
0
thanks to (
2.17
). In order to treat
J_{t}^{2}
let us preliminarily observe that if
k_{3}=0
, then
\sigma_{k,j}^{3,\varepsilon}\neq 0
if and only if
j=2
. Therefore we get

\displaystyle J_{t}^{2}

\displaystyle=\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k\in\mathbb{Z}^{3,N}_{0}\\
k_{3}=0,\hskip 8.19447ptj\in\{1,2\}\end{subarray}}\left\|\sigma_{k,j}^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla{{B}^{3,\varepsilon}_{t}}^{\prime}-{{B}^{\varepsilon}_{t}}^{\prime}\cdot\nabla\sigma_{k,j}^{3,\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}_{\varepsilon}

\displaystyle=\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k\in\mathbb{Z}^{3,N}_{0}\\
k_{3}=0,\hskip 8.19447ptj\in\{1,2\}\end{subarray}}\left\|\sigma_{k,j}^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla{{B}^{3,\varepsilon}_{t}}^{\prime}\right\|^{2}_{\varepsilon}+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k\in\mathbb{Z}^{3,N}_{0}\\
k_{3}=0,\hskip 8.19447ptj=2\end{subarray}}\left\|{{B}^{\varepsilon}_{t}}^{\prime}\cdot\nabla\sigma_{k,j}^{3,\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}_{\varepsilon}

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k\in\mathbb{Z}^{3,N}_{0}\\
k_{3}=0,\hskip 8.19447ptj\in\{1,2\}\end{subarray}}\left\|\sigma_{k,j}^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla{{B}^{3,\varepsilon}_{t}}^{\prime}\right\|^{2}_{\varepsilon}+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k_{H}\in\mathbb{Z}^{2}_{0}\\
N\leq\lvert k_{H}\rvert\leq 2N\end{subarray}}\frac{1}{C_{2,H}^{2}\lvert k_{H}\rvert^{\gamma-2}}\left\|{{B}^{\varepsilon}_{t}}^{\prime}\right\|^{2}_{\varepsilon}

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k\in\mathbb{Z}^{3,N}_{0}\\
k_{3}=0,\hskip 8.19447ptj\in\{1,2\}\end{subarray}}\left\|\sigma_{k,j}^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla{{B}^{3,\varepsilon}_{t}}^{\prime}\right\|^{2}_{\varepsilon}+\left(\frac{\zeta_{H,\gamma-2}}{N^{\gamma-4}C_{2,H}^{2}}+O(N^{3-\gamma})\right)\left\|{{B}^{\varepsilon}_{t}}^{\prime}\right\|^{2}_{\varepsilon}

(3.18)

\displaystyle=\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k\in\mathbb{Z}^{3,N}_{0}\\
k_{3}=0,\hskip 8.19447ptj\in\{1,2\}\end{subarray}}\left\|\sigma_{k,j}^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla{{B}^{3,\varepsilon}_{t}}^{\prime}\right\|^{2}_{\varepsilon}+\left(\frac{\zeta_{H,\gamma-2}}{N^{\gamma-4}C_{2,H}^{2}}+O(N^{3-\gamma})\right)\left\|\nabla{{A}^{\varepsilon}_{t}}^{\prime}\right\|^{2}_{\varepsilon},

exploiting relation (
3.1
) in the last equality.
The analysis of
J_{t}^{3}
is analogous to that of
J_{t}^{2}
and lead us to

(3.19)

\displaystyle J^{3}_{t}

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k\in\mathbb{Z}^{3,N}_{0}\\
k_{3}\neq 0,\hskip 8.19447ptj\in\{1,2\}\end{subarray}}\left\|\sigma_{k,j}^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla\overline{B^{3,\varepsilon}_{t}}\right\|^{2}_{\varepsilon}+\left(2\frac{\zeta_{\beta-2}\zeta_{H,0}}{N^{\beta-4}C_{V}^{2}}+O(N^{3-\beta})\right)\left\|\overline{B^{\varepsilon}_{t}}\right\|^{2}_{\varepsilon}.

For what concerns
J^{4}_{t}
, exploiting the fact that
N_{\varepsilon}
is a projection on
\dot{L}^{2}(\mathbb{T}^{3}_{\varepsilon})
we get easily

\displaystyle J_{t}^{4}

\displaystyle=\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k\in\mathbb{Z}^{3,N}_{0}\\
k_{3}\neq 0,\hskip 8.19447ptj\in\{1,2\}\end{subarray}}\left\|\sigma_{k,j}^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla{{B}^{3,\varepsilon}_{t}}^{\prime}-{{B}^{\varepsilon}_{t}}^{\prime}\cdot\nabla\sigma_{k,j}^{3,\varepsilon}\right\|_{\varepsilon}^{2}-\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k\in\mathbb{Z}^{3,N}_{0}\\
k_{3}\neq 0,\hskip 8.19447ptj\in\{1,2\}\end{subarray}}\left\|\overline{\sigma_{k,j}^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla{{B}^{3,\varepsilon}_{t}}^{\prime}-{{B}^{\varepsilon}_{t}}^{\prime}\cdot\nabla\sigma_{k,j}^{3,\varepsilon}}\right\|_{\varepsilon}^{2}

\displaystyle=\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k\in\mathbb{Z}^{3,N}_{0}\\
k_{3}\neq 0,\hskip 8.19447ptj\in\{1,2\}\end{subarray}}\left(\left\|\sigma_{k,j}^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla{{B}^{3,\varepsilon}_{t}}^{\prime}\right\|_{\varepsilon}^{2}+\left\|{{B}^{\varepsilon}_{t}}^{\prime}\cdot\nabla\sigma_{k,j}^{3,\varepsilon}\right\|_{\varepsilon}^{2}-\left\|\overline{\sigma_{k,j}^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla{{B}^{3,\varepsilon}_{t}}^{\prime}-{{B}^{\varepsilon}_{t}}^{\prime}\cdot\nabla\sigma_{k,j}^{3,\varepsilon}}\right\|_{\varepsilon}^{2}\right).

Let us analyze the second term in the last sum. We have by Hölder inequality and exploiting our definition of the
\sigma_{k,j}^{\varepsilon}

\displaystyle\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k\in\mathbb{Z}^{3,N}_{0}\\
k_{3}\neq 0,\hskip 8.19447ptj\in\{1,2\}\end{subarray}}\left\|{{B}^{\varepsilon}_{t}}^{\prime}\cdot\nabla\sigma_{k,j}^{3,\varepsilon}\right\|_{\varepsilon}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k\in\mathbb{Z}^{3,N}_{0}\\
k_{3}\neq 0,\hskip 8.19447ptj\in\{1,2\}\\
N\leq\lvert k_{H}\rvert\leq 2N\end{subarray}}\frac{1}{C_{V}^{2}\lvert k\rvert^{\beta-2}}\left\|{{B}^{\varepsilon}_{t}}^{\prime}\right\|_{\varepsilon}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\left(2\frac{\zeta_{\beta-2}\zeta_{H,0}}{N^{\beta-4}C_{V}^{2}}+O(N^{3-\beta})\right)\lVert\nabla{{A}^{\varepsilon}_{t}}^{\prime}\rVert_{\varepsilon}^{2}.

In conclusion we get

(3.20)

\displaystyle J_{t}^{4}

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k\in\mathbb{Z}^{3,N}_{0}\\
k_{3}\neq 0,\hskip 8.19447ptj\in\{1,2\}\end{subarray}}\left(\left\|\sigma_{k,j}^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla{{B}^{3,\varepsilon}_{t}}^{\prime}\right\|_{\varepsilon}^{2}-\left\|\overline{\sigma_{k,j}^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla{{B}^{3,\varepsilon}_{t}}^{\prime}-{{B}^{\varepsilon}_{t}}^{\prime}\cdot\nabla\sigma_{k,j}^{3,\varepsilon}}\right\|_{\varepsilon}^{2}\right)+\left(2\frac{\zeta_{\beta-2}\zeta_{H,0}}{N^{\beta-4}C_{V}^{2}}+O(N^{3-\beta})\right)\lVert\nabla{{A}^{\varepsilon}_{t}}^{\prime}\rVert_{\varepsilon}^{2}.

Exploiting the fact that for each
f,g\in L^{2}(\mathbb{T}^{3}_{\varepsilon})
we have
\langle\overline{f},g^{\prime}\rangle_{\varepsilon}=0
we get
J^{5}_{t}=0
. Indeed, thanks to the mirror symmetry property of the covariance function (
2.17
) and the definition of the
\sigma_{k,j}^{\varepsilon}
we have

\displaystyle J^{5}_{t}

\displaystyle=\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k\in\mathbb{Z}^{3,N}_{0}\\
k_{3}\neq 0,\hskip 8.19447ptj\in\{1,2\}\end{subarray}}\langle\sigma_{k,j}^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla\overline{B^{3,\varepsilon}_{t}}-\overline{B^{\varepsilon}_{t}}\cdot\nabla\sigma_{k,j}^{3,\varepsilon},\sigma_{-k,j}^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla{{B}^{3,\varepsilon}_{t}}^{\prime}-{{B}^{\varepsilon}_{t}}^{\prime}\cdot\nabla\sigma_{-k,j}^{3,\varepsilon}\rangle_{\varepsilon}

\displaystyle=\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k\in\mathbb{Z}^{3,N}_{0}\\
k_{3}\neq 0,\hskip 8.19447ptj\in\{1,2\}\end{subarray}}\langle\sigma_{k,j}^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla\overline{B^{3,\varepsilon}_{t}},\sigma_{-k,j}^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla{{B}^{3,\varepsilon}_{t}}^{\prime}\rangle_{\varepsilon}+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k\in\mathbb{Z}^{3,N}_{0}\\
k_{3}\neq 0,\hskip 8.19447ptj\in\{1,2\}\end{subarray}}\langle\overline{B^{\varepsilon}_{t}}\cdot\nabla\sigma_{k,j}^{3,\varepsilon},{{B}^{\varepsilon}_{t}}^{\prime}\cdot\nabla\sigma_{-k,j}^{3,\varepsilon}\rangle_{\varepsilon}

\displaystyle=\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k\in\mathbb{Z}^{3,N}_{0}\\
k_{3}\neq 0,\hskip 8.19447ptj\in\{1,2\}\end{subarray}}\left\lvert\theta_{k,j}^{\varepsilon}\right\rvert^{2}\langle a_{k,j}\cdot\nabla\overline{B^{3,\varepsilon}_{t}},a_{-k,j}\cdot\nabla{{B}^{3,\varepsilon}_{t}}^{\prime}\rangle_{\varepsilon}+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k\in\mathbb{Z}^{3,N}_{0}\\
k_{3}\neq 0,\hskip 8.19447ptj\in\{1,2\}\end{subarray}}\left\lvert\theta_{k,j}^{\varepsilon}\right\rvert^{2}\left(a_{k,j}^{3}\right)^{2}\langle\overline{B^{\varepsilon}_{t}}\cdot k,{{B}^{\varepsilon}_{t}}^{\prime}\cdot k\rangle

\displaystyle=0.

Lastly let us treat
J^{6}_{t}
: the term
-\langle\operatorname{div}_{H}\left(\mathcal{R}_{\varepsilon,\gamma}{{B}^{H,\varepsilon}_{t}}^{\prime}\right),{{B}^{3,\varepsilon}_{t}}^{\prime}\rangle_{\varepsilon}
can be estimated easily due to
2.10
and (
3.1
) getting by Cauchy-Schwarz inequality

\displaystyle-\langle\operatorname{div}_{H}\left(\mathcal{R}_{\varepsilon,\gamma}{{B}^{H,\varepsilon}_{t}}^{\prime}\right),{{B}^{3,\varepsilon}_{t}}^{\prime}\rangle_{\varepsilon}

\displaystyle=\langle\mathcal{R}_{\varepsilon,\gamma}{{B}^{H,\varepsilon}_{t}}^{\prime},\nabla_{H}{{B}^{3,\varepsilon}_{t}}^{\prime}\rangle_{\varepsilon}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2\sqrt{2}\lvert\rho\rvert\zeta^{N}_{H,\gamma/2}}{N^{\gamma/2-2}C_{1,H}C_{2,H}}\left\|\nabla{{B}^{3,\varepsilon}_{t}}^{\prime}\right\|_{\varepsilon}\left\|\nabla{{A}^{\varepsilon}_{t}}^{\prime}\right\|_{\varepsilon}.

The other terms can be treated easily by Cauchy-Schwartz inequality obtaining for each
\xi_{2}>0
and
k\in\mathbb{Z}_{0}^{3,N}
such that
k_{3}=0

\displaystyle\langle\sigma_{k,1}^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla{{B}^{3,\varepsilon}_{t}}^{\prime},\sigma_{-k,2}^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla{{B}^{3,\varepsilon}_{t}}^{\prime}-{{B}^{\varepsilon}_{t}}^{\prime}\cdot\nabla\sigma_{-k,2}^{3,\varepsilon}\rangle_{\varepsilon}\leq\frac{\left\|\sigma_{k,1}^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla{{B}^{3,\varepsilon}_{t}}^{\prime}\right\|_{\varepsilon}^{2}}{2\xi_{2}}+\frac{\xi_{2}\left\|\sigma_{k,2}^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla{{B}^{3,\varepsilon}_{t}}^{\prime}-{{B}^{\varepsilon}_{t}}^{\prime}\cdot\nabla\sigma_{k,2}^{3,\varepsilon}\right\|_{\varepsilon}^{2}}{2}.

So far, arguing when treating
J^{2}_{t}
, we proved that for each
\xi_{2}>0

\displaystyle J^{6}_{t}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2\sqrt{2}\lvert\rho\rvert\zeta^{N}_{H,\gamma/2}}{N^{\gamma/2-2}C_{1,H}C_{2,H}}\left\|\nabla{{B}^{3,\varepsilon}_{t}}^{\prime}\right\|_{\varepsilon}\left\|\nabla{{A}^{\varepsilon}_{t}}^{\prime}\right\|_{\varepsilon}+\frac{\lvert\rho\rvert}{\xi_{2}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k\in\mathbb{Z}^{3,N}_{0}\\
k_{3}=0,\hskip 8.19447ptj=1\end{subarray}}\left\|\sigma_{k,j}^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla{{B}^{3,\varepsilon}_{t}}^{\prime}\right\|^{2}_{\varepsilon}

(3.21)

\displaystyle+\lvert\rho\rvert\xi_{2}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k\in\mathbb{Z}^{3,N}_{0}\\
k_{3}=0,\hskip 8.19447ptj=2\end{subarray}}\left\|\sigma_{k,j}^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla{{B}^{3,\varepsilon}_{t}}^{\prime}\right\|^{2}_{\varepsilon}+\left(\frac{\lvert\rho\rvert\xi_{2}\zeta_{H,\gamma-2}}{N^{\gamma-4}C_{2,H}^{2}}+O(N^{3-\gamma})\right)\left\|\nabla{{A}^{\varepsilon}_{t}}^{\prime}\right\|^{2}_{\varepsilon}.

Let us analyze
\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k\in\mathbb{Z}^{3,N}_{0}\\
k_{3}=0,\hskip 8.19447ptj=1\end{subarray}}\left\|\sigma_{k,j}^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla{{B}^{3,\varepsilon}_{t}}^{\prime}\right\|^{2}_{\varepsilon}
and
\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k\in\mathbb{Z}^{3,N}_{0}\\
k_{3}=0,\hskip 8.19447ptj=2\end{subarray}}\left\|\sigma_{k,j}^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla{{B}^{3,\varepsilon}_{t}}^{\prime}\right\|^{2}_{\varepsilon}
. Arguing when treating
I^{6}_{t}
we have

\displaystyle\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k\in\mathbb{Z}^{3,N}_{0}\\
k_{3}=0,\hskip 8.19447ptj=1\end{subarray}}\left\|\sigma_{k,j}^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla{{B}^{3,\varepsilon}_{t}}^{\prime}\right\|^{2}_{\varepsilon}

\displaystyle\leq\left(\frac{\zeta_{H,2}}{C_{1,H}^{2}}+O(N^{-1})\right)\left\|\nabla{{B}^{3,\varepsilon}_{t}}^{\prime}\right\|_{\varepsilon}^{2},

\displaystyle\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k\in\mathbb{Z}^{3,N}_{0}\\
k_{3}=0,\hskip 8.19447ptj=2\end{subarray}}\left\|\sigma_{k,j}^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla{{B}^{3,\varepsilon}_{t}}^{\prime}\right\|^{2}_{\varepsilon}

\displaystyle\leq\left(\frac{\zeta_{H,\gamma}}{N^{\gamma-2}C_{1,H}^{2}}+O(N^{1-\gamma})\right)\left\|\nabla{{B}^{3,\varepsilon}_{t}}^{\prime}\right\|_{\varepsilon}^{2}.

In conclusion, applying Young’s inequality to the first term in (
3
), it holds

\displaystyle J^{6}_{t}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\lvert\rho\rvert}{\xi_{2}}\left(\frac{\zeta_{H,2}}{C_{1,H}^{2}}+\sqrt{2}\frac{\zeta_{H,\gamma/2}}{C_{1,H}C_{2,H}}+O(N^{-1})\right)\left\|\nabla{{B}^{3,\varepsilon}_{t}}^{\prime}\right\|_{\varepsilon}^{2}

(3.22)

\displaystyle+\lvert\rho\rvert\xi_{2}\left(\frac{\zeta_{H,\gamma-2}}{N^{\gamma-4}C_{2,H}^{2}}+\frac{\zeta_{H,\gamma/2}}{N^{\gamma-4}C_{1,H}C_{2,H}}+O(N^{3-\gamma})\right)\left\|\nabla{{A}^{\varepsilon}_{t}}^{\prime}\right\|^{2}_{\varepsilon}.

Combining (
3.17
), (
3
), (
3.19
), (
3.20
), (
3
), relation (
3.3
) follows.
∎

Lemma 3.4
.

Assuming
2.6
, for each
\xi_{3}>0
we have

\displaystyle d\left\|\overline{A^{3,\varepsilon}_{t}}\right\|_{\varepsilon}^{2}+2\eta\left\|\nabla\overline{A^{3,\varepsilon}_{t}}\right\|_{\varepsilon}^{2}dt

\displaystyle\leq 2\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k\in\mathbb{Z}^{3,N}_{0}\\
k_{3}\neq 0,\hskip 8.19447ptj\in\{1,2\}\end{subarray}}\langle\sigma_{k,j}^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla{{A}^{3,\varepsilon}_{t}}^{\prime}+\partial_{3}\sigma_{k,j}^{\varepsilon}\cdot{{A}^{\varepsilon}_{t}}^{\prime},\overline{A^{3,\varepsilon}_{t}}\rangle_{\varepsilon}dW^{k,j}_{t}

(3.23)

\displaystyle+\left(4\frac{\zeta_{\beta}\zeta_{H,0}}{N^{\beta-2}C_{V}^{2}}+O(N^{1-\beta})\right)\left\|\nabla{{A}^{3,\varepsilon}_{t}}^{\prime}\right\|_{\varepsilon}^{2}dt+\left(4\frac{\zeta_{\beta-2}\zeta_{H,0}}{N^{\beta-4}C_{V}^{2}}+O(N^{3-\beta})\right)\left\|{{A}^{\varepsilon}_{t}}^{\prime}\right\|_{\varepsilon}^{2}dt,

\displaystyle d\left\|\overline{B^{3,\varepsilon}_{t}}\right\|_{\varepsilon}^{2}+2\eta\left\|\nabla\overline{B^{3,\varepsilon}_{t}}\right\|_{\varepsilon}^{2}+2\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k\in\mathbb{Z}^{3,N}_{0}\\
k_{3}\neq 0,\hskip 8.19447ptj\in\{1,2\}\end{subarray}}\left\|\sigma_{k,j}^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla\overline{B^{3,\varepsilon}_{t}}\right\|_{\varepsilon}^{2}dt

\displaystyle\leq-2\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k\in\mathbb{Z}^{3,N}_{0}\\
k_{3}=0,\hskip 8.19447ptj\in\{1,2\}\end{subarray}}\langle\overline{B^{H,\varepsilon}_{t}}\cdot\nabla_{H}\sigma_{k,j}^{3,\varepsilon},\overline{B^{H,\varepsilon}_{3}}\rangle_{\varepsilon}dW^{k,j}_{t}+2\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k\in\mathbb{Z}^{3,N}_{0}\\
k_{3}\neq 0,\hskip 8.19447ptj\in\{1,2\}\end{subarray}}\langle\sigma_{k,j}^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla{{B}^{3,\varepsilon}_{t}}^{\prime}-{{B}^{\varepsilon}_{t}}^{\prime}\cdot\nabla\sigma_{k,j}^{3,\varepsilon},\overline{B^{3,\varepsilon}_{t}}\rangle_{\varepsilon}dW^{k,j}_{t}

\displaystyle+2\left(\frac{\zeta_{H,\gamma-2}}{N^{\gamma-4}C_{2,H}^{2}}+\lvert\rho\rvert\xi_{3}\frac{\zeta_{H,\gamma-2}}{N^{\gamma-4}C_{2,H}^{2}}+\sqrt{2}\lvert\rho\rvert\xi_{3}\frac{\zeta_{H,\gamma/2}}{N^{\gamma-4}C_{1,H}C_{2,H}}+O(N^{3-\gamma})\right)\left\|\nabla\overline{A^{3,\varepsilon}_{t}}\right\|_{\varepsilon}^{2}dt

(3.24)

\displaystyle+2\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k\in\mathbb{Z}^{3,N}_{0}\\
k_{3}\neq 0,\hskip 8.19447ptj\in\{1,2\}\end{subarray}}\left\|\overline{\sigma_{k,j}^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla{{B}^{3,\varepsilon}_{t}}^{\prime}-{{B}^{\varepsilon}_{t}}^{\prime}\cdot\nabla\sigma_{k,j}^{3,\varepsilon}}\right\|_{\varepsilon}^{2}dt+2\frac{\lvert\rho\rvert}{\xi_{3}}\left(\frac{\zeta_{H,2}}{C_{1,H}^{2}}+\sqrt{2}\frac{\zeta_{H,\gamma/2}}{C_{1,H}C_{2,H}}+O(N^{-1})\right)\left\|\nabla\overline{B^{3,\varepsilon}_{t}}\right\|_{\varepsilon}^{2}dt.

Proof.

First let us observe that thanks to (
2.1
) and
3.1

(3.25)

\displaystyle\overline{A^{3,\varepsilon}_{t}}

\displaystyle=-\nabla_{H}^{\perp}(-\Delta)^{-1}\overline{B^{H,\varepsilon}_{t}},\quad\left\|\overline{A^{3,\varepsilon}_{t}}\right\|_{\varepsilon}^{2}=\left\|\left(-\Delta\right)^{-1/2}\overline{B^{H,\varepsilon}_{t}}\right\|_{\varepsilon}^{2},\quad\left\|\nabla_{H}\overline{A^{3,\varepsilon}_{t}}\right\|_{\varepsilon}^{2}=\left\|\overline{B^{H,\varepsilon}_{t}}\right\|_{\varepsilon}^{2}.

Thus we apply Itô formula to
\left\|\left(-\Delta\right)^{-1/2}\overline{B^{H,\varepsilon}_{t}}\right\|_{\varepsilon}
obtaining due to
A.1

\displaystyle d\left\|\overline{A^{3,\varepsilon}_{t}}\right\|_{\varepsilon}^{2}=d\overline{W}_{t}+2(-L^{1}_{t}+L^{2}_{t}+L^{3}_{t}+L^{4}_{t})dt,

where we denote by

\displaystyle d\overline{W}_{t}

\displaystyle=2\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k\in\mathbb{Z}^{3,N}_{0}\\
k_{3}=0,\hskip 8.19447ptj\in\{1,2\}\end{subarray}}\langle\sigma_{k,j}^{H,\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla_{H}\overline{B^{H,\varepsilon}_{t}}-\overline{B^{H,\varepsilon}_{t}}\cdot\nabla_{H}\sigma_{k,j}^{H,\varepsilon},(-\Delta)^{-1}\overline{B^{H,\varepsilon}_{t}}\rangle_{\varepsilon}dW^{k,j}_{t}

\displaystyle+2\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k\in\mathbb{Z}^{3,N}_{0}\\
k_{3}\neq 0,\hskip 8.19447ptj\in\{1,2\}\end{subarray}}\langle\sigma_{k,j}^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla{{B}^{H,\varepsilon}_{t}}^{\prime}-{{B}^{\varepsilon}_{t}}^{\prime}\cdot\nabla\sigma_{k,j}^{H,\varepsilon},(-\Delta)^{-1}\overline{B^{H,\varepsilon}_{t}}\rangle_{\varepsilon}dW^{k,j}_{t},

\displaystyle L^{1}_{t}

\displaystyle=\eta\langle\nabla_{H}\overline{B^{H,\varepsilon}_{t}},(-\Delta)^{-1}\nabla_{H}\overline{B^{H,\varepsilon}_{t}}\rangle_{\varepsilon}+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k\in\mathbb{Z}^{3,N}_{0}\\
j\in\{1,2\}\end{subarray}}\langle\overline{B^{H,\varepsilon}_{t}},(-\Delta)^{-1}\operatorname{div}_{H}(\sigma_{k,j}^{H,\varepsilon}\otimes\sigma_{-k,j}^{H,\varepsilon}\nabla_{H}\overline{B^{H,\varepsilon}_{t}})\rangle_{\varepsilon},

\displaystyle L^{2}_{t}

\displaystyle=\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k\in\mathbb{Z}^{3,N}_{0}\\
k_{3}=0,\hskip 8.19447ptj\in\{1,2\}\end{subarray}}\langle\sigma_{k,j}^{H,\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla_{H}\overline{B^{H,\varepsilon}_{t}}-\overline{B^{H,\varepsilon}_{t}}\cdot\nabla_{H}\sigma_{k,j}^{H,\varepsilon},(-\Delta)^{-1}\left(\sigma_{-k,j}^{H,\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla_{H}\overline{B^{H,\varepsilon}_{t}}-\overline{B^{H,\varepsilon}_{t}}\cdot\nabla_{H}\sigma_{-k,j}^{H,\varepsilon}\right)\rangle_{\varepsilon}

\displaystyle L^{3}_{t}

\displaystyle=\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k\in\mathbb{Z}^{3,N}_{0}\\
k_{3}\neq 0,\hskip 8.19447ptj\in\{1,2\}\end{subarray}}\langle\overline{\sigma_{k,j}^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla{{B}^{H,\varepsilon}_{t}}^{\prime}-{{B}^{\varepsilon}_{t}}^{\prime}\cdot\nabla\sigma_{k,j}^{H,\varepsilon}},(-\Delta)^{-1}\overline{\sigma_{-k,j}^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla{{B}^{H,\varepsilon}_{t}}^{\prime}-{{B}^{\varepsilon}_{t}}^{\prime}\cdot\nabla\sigma_{-k,j}^{H,\varepsilon}}\rangle_{\varepsilon}

\displaystyle L^{4}_{t}

\displaystyle=\rho\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k\in\mathbb{Z}^{3,N}_{0}\\
k_{3}=0\end{subarray}}\langle\sigma_{k,H}^{1,\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla\overline{B^{H,\varepsilon}_{t}}-\overline{B^{H,\varepsilon}_{t}}\cdot\nabla\sigma_{k,1}^{\varepsilon},(-\Delta)^{-1}\left(\sigma_{-k,H}^{2,\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla\overline{B^{H,\varepsilon}_{t}}-\overline{B^{H,\varepsilon}_{t}}\cdot\nabla\sigma_{-k,2}^{\varepsilon}\right)\rangle_{\varepsilon}

\displaystyle+\rho\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k\in\mathbb{Z}^{3,N}_{0}\\
k_{3}=0\end{subarray}}\langle\sigma_{k,H}^{2,\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla\overline{B^{H,\varepsilon}_{t}}-\overline{B^{H,\varepsilon}_{t}}\cdot\nabla\sigma_{k,2}^{\varepsilon},(-\Delta)^{-1}\left(\sigma_{-k,H}^{1,\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla\overline{B^{H,\varepsilon}_{t}}-\overline{B^{H,\varepsilon}_{t}}\cdot\nabla\sigma_{-k,1}^{\varepsilon}\right)\rangle_{\varepsilon}.

Arguing analogously to the first part of
3.3
we can treat all the terms above. Thanks to (
3.2
) and (
3.5
) it holds

(3.26)

\displaystyle\sigma_{k,j}^{H,\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla_{H}\overline{B^{H,\varepsilon}_{t}}-\overline{B^{H,\varepsilon}_{t}}\cdot\nabla\sigma_{k,j}^{H,\varepsilon}

\displaystyle=\nabla_{H}^{\perp}(\sigma_{k,j}^{H,\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla_{H}\overline{A^{3,\varepsilon}_{t}})

\displaystyle\text{if }k_{3}=0,

(3.27)

\displaystyle\sigma_{k,j}^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla{{B}^{H,\varepsilon}_{t}}^{\prime}-{{B}^{\varepsilon}_{t}}^{\prime}\cdot\nabla\sigma_{k,j}^{H,\varepsilon}

\displaystyle=\left(\operatorname{curl}\left(\sigma_{k,j}^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla{{A}^{\varepsilon}_{t}}^{\prime}+D\sigma_{k,j}^{\varepsilon}{{A}^{\varepsilon}_{t}}^{\prime}\right)\right)^{H}

\displaystyle\text{if }k_{3}\neq 0.

In particular, considering the mean on the third direction of
\sigma_{k,j}^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla{{B}^{H,\varepsilon}_{t}}^{\prime}-{{B}^{\varepsilon}_{t}}^{\prime}\cdot\nabla\sigma_{k,j}^{H,\varepsilon}
, we have

(3.28)

\displaystyle\overline{\sigma_{k,j}^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla{{B}^{H,\varepsilon}_{t}}^{\prime}-{{B}^{\varepsilon}_{t}}^{\prime}\cdot\nabla\sigma_{k,j}^{H,\varepsilon}}

\displaystyle=-\nabla_{H}^{\perp}\left(\overline{\sigma_{k,j}^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla{{A}^{3,\varepsilon}_{t}}^{\prime}+\partial_{3}\sigma_{k,j}^{\varepsilon}\cdot{{A}^{\varepsilon}_{t}}^{\prime}}\right)

\displaystyle\text{if }k_{3}\neq 0.

Exploiting these relations we can obtain the required estimates. Let us start analyzing
d\overline{W}_{t}
.

\displaystyle d\overline{W}_{t}

\displaystyle=2\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k\in\mathbb{Z}^{3,N}_{0}\\
k_{3}=0,\hskip 8.19447ptj\in\{1,2\}\end{subarray}}\langle\nabla_{H}^{\perp}(\sigma_{k,j}^{H,\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla_{H}\overline{A^{3,\varepsilon}_{t}}),(-\Delta)^{-1}\nabla_{H}^{\perp}\overline{A^{3,\varepsilon}_{t}}\rangle_{\varepsilon}dW^{k,j}_{t}

\displaystyle+2\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k\in\mathbb{Z}^{3,N}_{0}\\
k_{3}\neq 0,\hskip 8.19447ptj\in\{1,2\}\end{subarray}}\langle\left(\operatorname{curl}\left(\sigma_{k,j}^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla{{A}^{\varepsilon}_{t}}^{\prime}+D\sigma_{k,j}^{\varepsilon}{{A}^{\varepsilon}_{t}}^{\prime}\right)\right)^{H},(-\Delta)^{-1}\nabla_{H}^{\perp}\overline{A^{3,\varepsilon}_{t}}\rangle_{\varepsilon}dW^{k,j}_{t}

\displaystyle=2\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k\in\mathbb{Z}^{3,N}_{0}\\
k_{3}=0,\hskip 8.19447ptj\in\{1,2\}\end{subarray}}\langle\sigma_{k,j}^{H,\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla_{H}\overline{A^{3,\varepsilon}_{t}},\overline{A^{3,\varepsilon}_{t}}\rangle_{\varepsilon}dW^{k,j}_{t}

\displaystyle+2\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k\in\mathbb{Z}^{3,N}_{0}\\
k_{3}\neq 0,\hskip 8.19447ptj\in\{1,2\}\end{subarray}}\langle\sigma_{k,j}^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla{{A}^{3,\varepsilon}_{t}}^{\prime}+\partial_{3}\sigma_{k,j}^{\varepsilon}\cdot{{A}^{\varepsilon}_{t}}^{\prime},\overline{A^{3,\varepsilon}_{t}}\rangle_{\varepsilon}dW^{k,j}_{t}

(3.29)

\displaystyle=2\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k\in\mathbb{Z}^{3,N}_{0}\\
k_{3}\neq 0,\hskip 8.19447ptj\in\{1,2\}\end{subarray}}\langle\sigma_{k,j}^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla{{A}^{3,\varepsilon}_{t}}^{\prime}+\partial_{3}\sigma_{k,j}^{\varepsilon}\cdot{{A}^{\varepsilon}_{t}}^{\prime},\overline{A^{3,\varepsilon}_{t}}\rangle_{\varepsilon}dW^{k,j}_{t}.

Moving to
L^{1}_{t}
we get easily, integrating by parts, that it holds

(3.30)

\displaystyle L^{1}_{t}

\displaystyle=\eta\left\|\nabla\overline{A^{3,\varepsilon}_{t}}\right\|_{\varepsilon}^{2}+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k\in\mathbb{Z}^{3,N}_{0}\\
j\in\{1,2\}\end{subarray}}\left\|\sigma_{k,j}^{H,\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla_{H}\overline{A^{3,\varepsilon}_{t}}\right\|_{\varepsilon}^{2}.

Concerning
L^{2}_{t}
we obtain by (
3.26
)

\displaystyle L^{2}_{t}

\displaystyle=\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k\in\mathbb{Z}^{3,N}_{0}\\
k_{3}=0,\hskip 8.19447ptj\in\{1,2\}\end{subarray}}\langle\nabla_{H}^{\perp}(\sigma_{k,j}^{H,\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla_{H}\overline{A^{3,\varepsilon}_{t}}),(-\Delta)^{-1}\nabla_{H}^{\perp}(\sigma_{-k,j}^{H,\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla_{H}\overline{A^{3,\varepsilon}_{t}})\rangle_{\varepsilon}

(3.31)

\displaystyle=\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k\in\mathbb{Z}^{3,N}_{0}\\
k_{3}=0,\hskip 8.19447ptj\in\{1,2\}\end{subarray}}\left\|\sigma_{k,j}^{H,\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla_{H}\overline{A^{3,\varepsilon}_{t}}\right\|_{\varepsilon}^{2}.

Thanks to (
3.28
) and computations analogous to the ones of
3.3
, we can treat
L^{3}_{t}
obtaining

\displaystyle L^{3}_{t}

\displaystyle=\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k\in\mathbb{Z}^{3,N}_{0}\\
k_{3}\neq 0,\hskip 8.19447ptj\in\{1,2\}\end{subarray}}\langle\overline{\sigma_{k,j}^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla{{B}^{H,\varepsilon}_{t}}^{\prime}-{{B}^{\varepsilon}_{t}}^{\prime}\cdot\nabla\sigma_{k,j}^{H,\varepsilon}},(-\Delta)^{-1}\overline{\sigma_{-k,j}^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla{{B}^{H,\varepsilon}_{t}}^{\prime}-{{B}^{\varepsilon}_{t}}^{\prime}\cdot\nabla\sigma_{-k,j}^{H,\varepsilon}}\rangle_{\varepsilon}

\displaystyle=\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k\in\mathbb{Z}^{3,N}_{0}\\
k_{3}\neq 0,\hskip 8.19447ptj\in\{1,2\}\end{subarray}}\langle\nabla_{H}^{\perp}\left(\overline{\sigma_{k,j}^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla{{A}^{3,\varepsilon}_{t}}^{\prime}+\partial_{3}\sigma_{k,j}^{\varepsilon}\cdot{{A}^{\varepsilon}_{t}}^{\prime}}\right),(-\Delta)^{-1}\nabla_{H}^{\perp}\left(\overline{\sigma_{-k,j}^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla{{A}^{3,\varepsilon}_{t}}^{\prime}+\partial_{3}\sigma_{-k,j}^{\varepsilon}\cdot{{A}^{\varepsilon}_{t}}^{\prime}}\right)\rangle_{\varepsilon}

\displaystyle=\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k\in\mathbb{Z}^{3,N}_{0}\\
k_{3}\neq 0,\hskip 8.19447ptj\in\{1,2\}\end{subarray}}\left\|\overline{\sigma_{k,j}^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla{{A}^{3,\varepsilon}_{t}}^{\prime}+\partial_{3}\sigma_{k,j}^{\varepsilon}\cdot{{A}^{\varepsilon}_{t}}^{\prime}}\right\|_{\varepsilon}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k\in\mathbb{Z}^{3,N}_{0}\\
k_{3}\neq 0,\hskip 8.19447ptj\in\{1,2\}\end{subarray}}\left\|\sigma_{k,j}^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla{{A}^{3,\varepsilon}_{t}}^{\prime}+\partial_{3}\sigma_{k,j}^{\varepsilon}\cdot{{A}^{\varepsilon}_{t}}^{\prime}\right\|_{\varepsilon}^{2}

(3.32)

\displaystyle\leq\left(2\frac{\zeta_{\beta}\zeta_{H,0}}{N^{\beta-2}C_{V}^{2}}+O(N^{1-\beta})\right)\left\|\nabla{{A}^{3,\varepsilon}_{t}}^{\prime}\right\|_{\varepsilon}^{2}+\left(2\frac{\zeta_{\beta-2}\zeta_{H,0}}{N^{\beta-4}C_{V}^{2}}+O(N^{3-\beta})\right)\left\|{{A}^{\varepsilon}_{t}}^{\prime}\right\|_{\varepsilon}^{2}.

Lastly
L^{4}_{t}=0
thanks to
3.2
. Indeed for each
k\in\mathbb{Z}_{0}^{3,N}
such that
k_{3}=0
it holds

\displaystyle\langle\sigma_{k,H}^{1,\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla\overline{B^{H,\varepsilon}_{t}}-\overline{B^{H,\varepsilon}_{t}}\cdot\nabla\sigma_{k,1}^{\varepsilon},(-\Delta)^{-1}\left(\sigma_{-k,H}^{2,\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla\overline{B^{H,\varepsilon}_{t}}-\overline{B^{H,\varepsilon}_{t}}\cdot\nabla\sigma_{-k,2}^{\varepsilon}\right)\rangle_{\varepsilon}

\displaystyle=\langle\operatorname{curl}\left(\sigma_{k,1}^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla\overline{A^{3,\varepsilon}_{t}}\right),(-\Delta)^{-1}\operatorname{curl}\left(\sigma_{-k,2}^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla\overline{A^{3,\varepsilon}_{t}}\right)\rangle_{\varepsilon}

\displaystyle=\langle\sigma_{k,1}^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla\overline{A^{3,\varepsilon}_{t}},\sigma_{-k,2}^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla\overline{A^{3,\varepsilon}_{t}}\rangle_{\varepsilon}=0.

Relation (
3.4
) then follows combining (
3
), (
3.30
), (
3
) and (
3
).

Now we move to the analysis of
\left\|\overline{B^{3,\varepsilon}_{t}}\right\|_{\varepsilon}^{2}
which is similar and easier to the previous one. First by Itô formula we have

\displaystyle d\left\|\overline{B^{3,\varepsilon}_{t}}\right\|_{\varepsilon}^{2}=d\hat{W}_{t}+2(-H^{1}_{t}+H^{2}_{t}+H^{3}_{t}+H^{4}_{t})dt,

where we denote by

\displaystyle d\hat{W}_{t}

\displaystyle=2\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k\in\mathbb{Z}^{3,N}_{0}\\
k_{3}=0,\hskip 8.19447ptj\in\{1,2\}\end{subarray}}\langle\sigma_{k,j}^{H,\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla_{H}\overline{B^{3,\varepsilon}_{t}}-\overline{B^{H,\varepsilon}_{t}}\cdot\nabla_{H}\sigma_{k,j}^{3,\varepsilon},\overline{B^{3,\varepsilon}_{t}}\rangle_{\varepsilon}dW^{k,j}_{t}

\displaystyle+2\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k\in\mathbb{Z}^{3,N}_{0}\\
k_{3}\neq 0,\hskip 8.19447ptj\in\{1,2\}\end{subarray}}\langle\sigma_{k,j}^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla{{B}^{3,\varepsilon}_{t}}^{\prime}-{{B}^{\varepsilon}_{t}}^{\prime}\cdot\nabla\sigma_{k,j}^{3,\varepsilon},\overline{B^{3,\varepsilon}_{t}}\rangle_{\varepsilon}dW^{k,j}_{t},

\displaystyle H^{1}_{t}

\displaystyle=\eta\left\|\nabla_{H}\overline{B^{3,\varepsilon}_{t}}\right\|_{\varepsilon}^{2}+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k\in\mathbb{Z}^{3,N}_{0}\\
j\in\{1,2\}\end{subarray}}\left\|\sigma_{k,j}^{H,\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla_{H}\overline{B^{3,\varepsilon}_{t}}\right\|_{\varepsilon}^{2},

\displaystyle H^{2}_{t}

\displaystyle=\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k\in\mathbb{Z}^{3,N}_{0}\\
k_{3}=0,\hskip 8.19447ptj\in\{1,2\}\end{subarray}}\langle\sigma_{k,j}^{H,\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla_{H}\overline{B^{3,\varepsilon}_{t}}-\overline{B^{H,\varepsilon}_{t}}\cdot\nabla_{H}\sigma_{k,j}^{3,\varepsilon},\sigma_{-k,j}^{H,\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla_{H}\overline{B^{3,\varepsilon}_{t}}-\overline{B^{H,\varepsilon}_{t}}\cdot\nabla_{H}\sigma_{-k,j}^{3,\varepsilon}\rangle_{\varepsilon},

\displaystyle H^{3}_{t}

\displaystyle=\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k\in\mathbb{Z}^{3,N}_{0}\\
k_{3}\neq 0,\hskip 8.19447ptj\in\{1,2\}\end{subarray}}\langle\overline{\sigma_{k,j}^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla{{B}^{3,\varepsilon}_{t}}^{\prime}-{{B}^{\varepsilon}_{t}}^{\prime}\cdot\nabla\sigma_{k,j}^{3,\varepsilon}},\overline{\sigma_{-k,j}^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla{{B}^{3,\varepsilon}_{t}}^{\prime}-{{B}^{\varepsilon}_{t}}^{\prime}\cdot\nabla\sigma_{-k,j}^{3,\varepsilon}}\rangle_{\varepsilon},

\displaystyle H^{4}_{t}

\displaystyle=-\langle\operatorname{div}_{H}\left(\mathcal{R}_{\varepsilon,\gamma}\overline{B^{H,\varepsilon}_{t}}\right),\overline{B^{3,\varepsilon}_{t}}\rangle_{\varepsilon}-\rho\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k\in\mathbb{Z}^{3,N}_{0}\\
k_{3}=0\end{subarray}}\langle\sigma_{k,1}^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla\overline{B^{3,\varepsilon}_{t}},\overline{B^{\varepsilon}_{t}}\cdot\nabla\sigma_{-k,2}^{3,\varepsilon}\rangle_{\varepsilon}+\langle\overline{B^{\varepsilon}_{t}}\cdot\nabla\sigma_{k,2}^{3,\varepsilon},\sigma_{-k,1}^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla\overline{B^{3,\varepsilon}_{t}}\rangle_{\varepsilon}.

Analogously to the second part of
3.3
we can treat all the terms above. Let us start from
d\hat{W}_{t}
. Since the
\sigma_{k,j}^{\varepsilon}
are divergence free we get immediately

(3.33)

\displaystyle d\hat{W}_{t}

\displaystyle=-2\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k\in\mathbb{Z}^{3,N}_{0}\\
k_{3}=0,\hskip 8.19447ptj\in\{1,2\}\end{subarray}}\langle\overline{B^{H,\varepsilon}_{t}}\cdot\nabla_{H}\sigma_{k,j}^{3,\varepsilon},\overline{B^{3,\varepsilon}_{t}}\rangle_{\varepsilon}dW^{k,j}_{t}+2\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k\in\mathbb{Z}^{3,N}_{0}\\
k_{3}\neq 0,\hskip 8.19447ptj\in\{1,2\}\end{subarray}}\langle\sigma_{k,j}^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla{{B}^{3,\varepsilon}_{t}}^{\prime}-{{B}^{\varepsilon}_{t}}^{\prime}\cdot\nabla\sigma_{k,j}^{3,\varepsilon},\overline{B^{3,\varepsilon}_{t}}\rangle_{\varepsilon}dW^{k,j}_{t}.

Thanks to (
2.17
) we get easily by Hölder inequality

\displaystyle H^{2}_{t}

\displaystyle=\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k\in\mathbb{Z}^{3,N}_{0}\\
k_{3}=0,\hskip 8.19447ptj\in\{1,2\}\end{subarray}}\left\|\sigma_{k,j}^{H,\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla_{H}\overline{B^{3,\varepsilon}_{t}}\right\|_{\varepsilon}^{2}+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k\in\mathbb{Z}^{3,N}_{0}\\
k_{3}=0,\hskip 8.19447ptj\in\{1,2\}\end{subarray}}\left\|\overline{B^{H,\varepsilon}_{t}}\cdot\nabla_{H}\sigma_{k,j}^{3,\varepsilon}\right\|_{\varepsilon}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k\in\mathbb{Z}^{3,N}_{0}\\
k_{3}=0,\hskip 8.19447ptj\in\{1,2\}\end{subarray}}\left\|\sigma_{k,j}^{H,\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla_{H}\overline{B^{3,\varepsilon}_{t}}\right\|_{\varepsilon}^{2}+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k\in\mathbb{Z}^{3,N}_{0}\\
k_{3}=0,\hskip 8.19447ptj=2\end{subarray}}\left\lvert\theta_{k,j}^{\varepsilon}\right\rvert^{2}\lvert k\rvert^{2}\left\|\overline{B^{H,\varepsilon}_{t}}\right\|_{\varepsilon}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k\in\mathbb{Z}^{3,N}_{0}\\
k_{3}=0,\hskip 8.19447ptj\in\{1,2\}\end{subarray}}\left\|\sigma_{k,j}^{H,\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla_{H}\overline{B^{3,\varepsilon}_{t}}\right\|_{\varepsilon}^{2}+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k_{H}\in\mathbb{Z}^{2}_{0}\\
N\leq\lvert k_{H}\rvert\leq 2N\end{subarray}}\frac{1}{C_{2,H}^{2}\lvert k\rvert^{\gamma-2}}\left\|\nabla\overline{A^{3,\varepsilon}_{t}}\right\|_{\varepsilon}^{2}

(3.34)

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k\in\mathbb{Z}^{3,N}_{0}\\
k_{3}=0,\hskip 8.19447ptj\in\{1,2\}\end{subarray}}\left\|\sigma_{k,j}^{H,\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla_{H}\overline{B^{3,\varepsilon}_{t}}\right\|_{\varepsilon}^{2}+\left(\frac{\zeta_{H,\gamma-2}}{N^{\gamma-4}C_{2,H}^{2}}+O(N^{3-\gamma})\right)\left\|\nabla\overline{A^{3,\varepsilon}_{t}}\right\|_{\varepsilon}^{2}.

We rewrite
H_{t}^{3}
as

(3.35)

\displaystyle H^{3}_{t}

\displaystyle=\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k\in\mathbb{Z}^{3,N}_{0}\\
k_{3}\neq 0,\hskip 8.19447ptj\in\{1,2\}\end{subarray}}\left\|\overline{\sigma_{k,j}^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla{{B}^{3,\varepsilon}_{t}}^{\prime}-{{B}^{\varepsilon}_{t}}^{\prime}\cdot\nabla\sigma_{k,j}^{3,\varepsilon}}\right\|_{\varepsilon}^{2}.

Lastly let us treat
H^{4}_{t}
: the term
-\langle\operatorname{div}_{H}\left(\mathcal{R}_{\varepsilon,\gamma}\overline{B^{H,\varepsilon}_{t}}\right),\overline{B^{3,\varepsilon}_{t}}\rangle_{\varepsilon}
can be estimated easily due to
2.10
and (
3.2
) obtaining by Cauchy-Schwarz inequality

\displaystyle-\langle\operatorname{div}_{H}\left(\mathcal{R}_{\varepsilon,\gamma}{{B}^{H,\varepsilon}_{t}}^{\prime}\right),{{B}^{3,\varepsilon}_{t}}^{\prime}\rangle_{\varepsilon}

\displaystyle=-\langle\mathcal{R}_{\varepsilon,\gamma}\cdot\nabla^{\perp}_{H}\overline{A^{3,\varepsilon}_{t}},\nabla_{H}\overline{B^{3,\varepsilon}_{t}}\rangle_{\varepsilon}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2\sqrt{2}\lvert\rho\rvert\zeta^{N}_{H,\gamma/2}}{N^{\gamma/2-2}C_{1,H}C_{2,H}}\left\|\nabla\overline{B^{3,\varepsilon}_{t}}\right\|_{\varepsilon}\left\|\nabla\overline{A^{3,\varepsilon}_{t}}\right\|_{\varepsilon}.

The other terms can be treated easily by Cauchy-Schwartz inequality. Indeed, for each
\xi_{3}>0
and
k\in\mathbb{Z}_{0}^{3,N}
such that
k_{3}=0
it holds

\displaystyle\langle\sigma_{k,1}^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla\overline{B^{3,\varepsilon}_{t}},\overline{B^{\varepsilon}_{t}}\cdot\nabla\sigma_{-k,2}^{3,\varepsilon}\rangle_{\varepsilon}\leq\frac{\left\|\sigma_{k,1}^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla\overline{B^{3,\varepsilon}_{t}}\right\|_{\varepsilon}^{2}}{2\xi_{3}}+\frac{\xi_{3}\left\|\overline{B^{H,\varepsilon}_{t}}\cdot\nabla_{H}\sigma_{k,2}^{3,\varepsilon}\right\|_{\varepsilon}^{2}}{2}.

In conclusion arguing as when treating
H^{2}_{t}
, so far, we have proved that for each
\xi_{3}>0

\displaystyle H^{4}_{t}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2\sqrt{2}\lvert\rho\rvert\zeta^{N}_{H,\gamma/2}}{N^{\gamma/2-2}C_{1,H}C_{2,H}}\left\|\nabla\overline{B^{3,\varepsilon}_{t}}\right\|_{\varepsilon}\left\|\nabla\overline{A^{3,\varepsilon}_{t}}\right\|_{\varepsilon}+\frac{\lvert\rho\rvert}{\xi_{3}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k\in\mathbb{Z}^{3,N}_{0}\\
k_{3}=0,\hskip 8.19447ptj=1\end{subarray}}\left\|\sigma_{k,j}^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla\overline{B^{3,\varepsilon}_{t}}\right\|^{2}_{\varepsilon}

(3.36)

\displaystyle+\lvert\rho\rvert\xi_{3}\left(\frac{\zeta_{H,\gamma-2}}{N^{\gamma-4}C_{2,H}^{2}}+O(N^{3-\gamma})\right)\left\|\nabla\overline{A^{3,\varepsilon}_{t}}\right\|_{\varepsilon}^{2}.

Let us analyze
\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k\in\mathbb{Z}^{3,N}_{0}\\
k_{3}=0,\hskip 8.19447ptj=1\end{subarray}}\left\|\sigma_{k,j}^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla\overline{B^{3,\varepsilon}_{t}}\right\|^{2}_{\varepsilon}
. Arguing as in the second part of
3.3
we have

\displaystyle\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k\in\mathbb{Z}^{3,N}_{0}\\
k_{3}=0,\hskip 8.19447ptj=1\end{subarray}}\left\|\sigma_{k,j}^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla\overline{B^{3,\varepsilon}_{t}}\right\|^{2}_{\varepsilon}

\displaystyle\leq\left(\frac{\zeta_{H,2}}{C_{1,H}^{2}}+O(N^{-1})\right)\left\|\nabla\overline{B^{3,\varepsilon}_{t}}\right\|_{\varepsilon}^{2}.

In conclusion, applying Young’s inequality to the first term of (
3
), it holds

\displaystyle H^{4}_{t}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\lvert\rho\rvert}{\xi_{3}}\left(\frac{\zeta_{H,2}}{C_{1,H}^{2}}+\sqrt{2}\frac{\zeta_{H,\gamma/2}}{C_{1,H}C_{2,H}}+O(N^{-1})\right)\left\|\nabla\overline{B^{3,\varepsilon}_{t}}\right\|_{\varepsilon}^{2}

(3.37)

\displaystyle+\lvert\rho\rvert\xi_{3}\left(\frac{\zeta_{H,\gamma-2}}{N^{\gamma-4}C_{2,H}^{2}}+\sqrt{2}\frac{\zeta_{H,\gamma/2}}{N^{\gamma-4}C_{1,H}C_{2,H}}+O(N^{3-\gamma})\right)\left\|\nabla\overline{A^{3,\varepsilon}_{t}}\right\|_{\varepsilon}^{2}.

Combining (
3.33
), (
3
), (
3.35
) and (
3
) we get relation (
3.4
).
∎

3.3
and
3.4
imply the following version of (
2.36
):

Proposition 3.5
.

Assuming
2.6
, for
N>0
large enough

(3.38)

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\sup_{t\in[0,T]}\left\|\overline{A^{3,\varepsilon}_{t}}\right\|_{\varepsilon}^{2}\right]+\mathbb{E}\left[\int_{0}^{T}\left\|\nabla\overline{A^{3,\varepsilon}_{s}}\right\|_{\varepsilon}^{2}ds\right]

\displaystyle\lesssim{\left\|B_{0}^{3,\varepsilon}\right\|_{\varepsilon}^{2}}+{\left\|\overline{A^{3,\varepsilon}_{0}}\right\|_{\varepsilon}^{2}}+{\left\|{{A}^{\varepsilon}_{0}}^{\prime}\right\|_{\varepsilon}^{2}}.

(3.39)

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\sup_{t\in[0,T]}\left\|\overline{B^{3,\varepsilon}_{t}}\right\|_{\varepsilon}^{2}\right]+\mathbb{E}\left[\int_{0}^{T}\left\|\nabla\overline{B^{3,\varepsilon}_{s}}\right\|_{\varepsilon}^{2}ds\right]

\displaystyle\lesssim{\left\|B_{0}^{3,\varepsilon}\right\|_{\varepsilon}^{2}}+{\left\|\overline{A^{3,\varepsilon}_{0}}\right\|_{\varepsilon}^{2}}+{\left\|{{A}^{\varepsilon}_{0}}^{\prime}\right\|_{\varepsilon}^{2}}.

(3.40)

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\sup_{t\in[0,T]}\left\|{{A}^{\varepsilon}_{t}}^{\prime}\right\|_{\varepsilon}^{2}\right]+\mathbb{E}\left[\int_{0}^{T}\left\|\nabla{{A}^{\varepsilon}_{s}}^{\prime}\right\|_{\varepsilon}^{2}ds\right]

\displaystyle\lesssim{\left\|B_{0}^{3,\varepsilon}\right\|_{\varepsilon}^{2}}+{\left\|\overline{A^{3,\varepsilon}_{0}}\right\|_{\varepsilon}^{2}}+{\left\|{{A}^{\varepsilon}_{0}}^{\prime}\right\|_{\varepsilon}^{2}}.

(3.41)

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\sup_{t\in[0,T]}\left\|{{B}^{3,\varepsilon}_{t}}^{\prime}\right\|_{\varepsilon}^{2}\right]+\mathbb{E}\left[\int_{0}^{T}\left\|\nabla{{B}^{3,\varepsilon}_{s}}^{\prime}\right\|_{\varepsilon}^{2}ds\right]

\displaystyle\lesssim{\left\|B_{0}^{3,\varepsilon}\right\|_{\varepsilon}^{2}}+{\left\|\overline{A^{3,\varepsilon}_{0}}\right\|_{\varepsilon}^{2}}+{\left\|{{A}^{\varepsilon}_{0}}^{\prime}\right\|_{\varepsilon}^{2}}.

The hidden constants above all depend only on
\eta,\beta,\gamma,C_{V},C_{1,H},C_{2,H},\lvert\rho\rvert,T
.

Proof.

In order to simplify the notation, we will denote by
C_{par}
several constants depending from
\eta
,
\lvert\rho\rvert
,
\gamma
,
\beta
,
C_{1,H}
,
C_{2,H}
,
C_{V}
,
T
, possibly changing their values line by line but independent from
N
in this proof. Let us introduce a new parameter
\eta_{aux}
such that

\displaystyle\frac{\zeta_{H,0}}{C_{1,H}^{2}}<\eta_{aux}<\eta.

Recalling the discussion at the beginning of
section 3
, it is easy to show that for each
\varepsilon>0
the stochastic integrals appearing in (
3.3
), (
3.3
), (
3.4
), (
3.4
) are true martingales.
Let us start considering the expected value of (
3.3
). We get for each
t\in[0,T]

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|{{A}^{\varepsilon}_{t}}^{\prime}\right\|_{\varepsilon}^{2}\right]+2\eta\mathbb{E}\left[\int_{0}^{t}\left\|\nabla{{A}^{\varepsilon}_{s}}^{\prime}\right\|^{2}_{\varepsilon}ds\right]

\displaystyle\leq{\left\|{{A}^{\varepsilon}_{0}}^{\prime}\right\|_{\varepsilon}^{2}}+\left(6\frac{\zeta_{\beta}\zeta_{H,0}}{N^{\beta-2}C_{V}^{2}}+O(N^{1-\beta})\right)\mathbb{E}\left[\int_{0}^{t}\lVert\nabla\overline{A^{\varepsilon}_{s}}\rVert_{\varepsilon}^{2}ds\right]+\left(6\frac{\zeta_{\beta-2}\zeta_{H,0}}{N^{\beta-4}C_{V}^{2}}+O(N^{3-\beta})\right)\mathbb{E}\left[\int_{0}^{t}\left\|\overline{A^{\varepsilon}_{s}}\right\|_{\varepsilon}^{2}ds\right]

\displaystyle+2\left(\frac{\zeta_{H,0}}{C_{1,H}^{2}}+\frac{\lvert\rho\rvert}{\xi_{1}}\frac{\left(\zeta_{H,0}+\zeta_{H,2}\right)}{C_{1,H}^{2}}+O(N^{-1})\right)\mathbb{E}\left[\int_{0}^{t}\left\|\nabla{{A}^{\varepsilon}_{s}}^{\prime}\right\|^{2}_{\varepsilon}ds\right].

Therefore, if we choose
\xi_{1}
large enough, there exists
N_{0}\in\mathbb{N}
such that for each
N\geq N_{0}
it holds

(3.42)

\displaystyle\frac{\zeta_{H,0}}{C_{1,H}^{2}}+\frac{\lvert\rho\rvert}{\xi_{1}}\frac{\left(\zeta_{H,0}+\zeta_{H,2}\right)}{C_{1,H}^{2}}+O(N^{-1})\leq\eta_{aux}

and we get

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|{{A}^{\varepsilon}_{t}}^{\prime}\right\|_{\varepsilon}^{2}\right]+(\eta-\eta_{aux})\mathbb{E}\left[\int_{0}^{t}\left\|\nabla{{A}^{\varepsilon}_{s}}^{\prime}\right\|^{2}_{\varepsilon}ds\right]

\displaystyle\leq{\left\|{{A}^{\varepsilon}_{0}}^{\prime}\right\|_{\varepsilon}^{2}}+\left(6\frac{\zeta_{\beta}\zeta_{H,0}}{N^{\beta-2}C_{V}^{2}}+O(N^{1-\beta})\right)\mathbb{E}\left[\int_{0}^{t}\lVert\nabla\overline{A^{\varepsilon}_{s}}\rVert_{\varepsilon}^{2}ds\right]

(3.43)

\displaystyle+\left(6\frac{\zeta_{\beta-2}\zeta_{H,0}}{N^{\beta-4}C_{V}^{2}}+O(N^{3-\beta})\right)\mathbb{E}\left[\int_{0}^{t}\left\|\overline{A^{\varepsilon}_{s}}\right\|_{\varepsilon}^{2}ds\right].

Now we take the expected value of (
3.4
) at time
t\in[0,T]
obtaining by Poincaré inequality (
2.5
)

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\overline{A^{3,\varepsilon}_{t}}\right\|_{\varepsilon}^{2}\right]+2\eta\mathbb{E}\left[\int_{0}^{t}\left\|\nabla\overline{A^{3,\varepsilon}_{s}}\right\|_{\varepsilon}^{2}ds\right]

\displaystyle\leq{\left\|\overline{A^{3,\varepsilon}_{0}}\right\|_{\varepsilon}^{2}}+4\left(\frac{\zeta_{\beta}\zeta_{H,0}}{N^{\beta-2}C_{V}^{2}}+\frac{\zeta_{\beta-2}\zeta_{H,0}}{N^{\beta-2}C_{V}^{2}}+O(N^{1-\beta})\right)\mathbb{E}\left[\int_{0}^{t}\left\|\nabla{{A}^{\varepsilon}_{s}}^{\prime}\right\|_{\varepsilon}^{2}ds\right].

Plugging (
3
) in the relation above we get

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\overline{A^{3,\varepsilon}_{t}}\right\|_{\varepsilon}^{2}\right]+2\eta\mathbb{E}\left[\int_{0}^{t}\left\|\nabla\overline{A^{3,\varepsilon}_{s}}\right\|_{\varepsilon}^{2}ds\right]

\displaystyle\leq{\left\|\overline{A^{3,\varepsilon}_{0}}\right\|_{\varepsilon}^{2}}+\left(\frac{C_{par}}{N^{\beta-2}}+O(N^{1-\beta})\right)\times

(3.44)

\displaystyle\quad\quad\left(\left\|{{A}^{\varepsilon}_{0}}^{\prime}\right\|_{\varepsilon}^{2}+\left(\frac{C_{par}}{N^{\beta-2}}+O(N^{1-\beta})\right)\mathbb{E}\left[\int_{0}^{t}\lVert\nabla\overline{A^{\varepsilon}_{s}}\rVert_{\varepsilon}^{2}ds\right]+\left(\frac{C_{par}}{N^{\beta-4}}+O(N^{3-\beta})\right)\mathbb{E}\left[\int_{0}^{t}\left\|\overline{A^{\varepsilon}_{s}}\right\|_{\varepsilon}^{2}ds\right]\right).

In order to simplify the expression above, let us observe that since
\overline{A^{\varepsilon}_{t}}
is independent of the third component, Poincaré inequality with constant equal to
1
holds. Therefore we have

(3.45)

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\int_{0}^{t}\left\|\overline{A^{\varepsilon}_{s}}\right\|_{\varepsilon}^{2}ds\right]\leq\mathbb{E}\left[\int_{0}^{t}\left\|\nabla\overline{A^{\varepsilon}_{s}}\right\|_{\varepsilon}^{2}ds\right].

Secondly, thanks to
3.1
it holds

(3.46)

\displaystyle\left\|\nabla\overline{A^{\varepsilon}_{t}}\right\|_{\varepsilon}^{2}

\displaystyle=\left\|\overline{B^{\varepsilon}_{t}}\right\|_{\varepsilon}^{2}=\left\|\overline{B^{3,\varepsilon}_{t}}\right\|_{\varepsilon}^{2}+\left\|\overline{B^{H,\varepsilon}_{t}}\right\|_{\varepsilon}^{2}=\left\|\overline{B^{3,\varepsilon}_{t}}\right\|_{\varepsilon}^{2}+\left\|\nabla\overline{A^{3,\varepsilon}_{t}}\right\|_{\varepsilon}^{2},

(3.47)

\displaystyle\left\|\overline{A^{\varepsilon}_{t}}\right\|_{\varepsilon}^{2}

\displaystyle=\left\|(-\Delta)^{-1/2}\overline{B^{\varepsilon}_{t}}\right\|_{\varepsilon}^{2}=\left\|(-\Delta)^{-1/2}\overline{B^{3,\varepsilon}_{t}}\right\|_{\varepsilon}^{2}+\left\|(-\Delta)^{-1/2}\overline{B^{H,\varepsilon}_{t}}\right\|_{\varepsilon}^{2}\leq\left\|\overline{B^{3,\varepsilon}_{t}}\right\|_{\varepsilon}^{2}+\left\|\overline{A^{3,\varepsilon}_{t}}\right\|_{\varepsilon}^{2}.

Combining (
3.45
) and (
3.46
) in relation (
3
) we get

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\overline{A^{3,\varepsilon}_{t}}\right\|_{\varepsilon}^{2}\right]+2\eta\mathbb{E}\left[\int_{0}^{t}\left\|\nabla\overline{A^{3,\varepsilon}_{s}}\right\|_{\varepsilon}^{2}ds\right]

\displaystyle\leq{\left\|\overline{A^{3,\varepsilon}_{0}}\right\|_{\varepsilon}^{2}}+\frac{C_{par}}{N^{2-\beta}}{\left\|{{A}^{\varepsilon}_{0}}^{\prime}\right\|_{\varepsilon}^{2}}

\displaystyle+\left(\frac{C_{par}}{N^{\beta-2}}+O(N^{1-\beta})\right)\mathbb{E}\left[\int_{0}^{t}\lVert\nabla\overline{A^{3,\varepsilon}_{s}}\rVert_{\varepsilon}^{2}ds\right]

\displaystyle+\left(\frac{C_{par}}{N^{\beta-2}}+O(N^{1-\beta})\right)\mathbb{E}\left[\int_{0}^{t}\lVert\nabla\overline{B^{3,\varepsilon}_{s}}\rVert_{\varepsilon}^{2}ds\right].

Therefore, choosing

.
